<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47340079' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47340079' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(1) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/newsguidelines.html#generated' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Don&#x27;t post generated/AI-edited comments. HN is for conversation between humans</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不要发布生成的 AI 编辑评论。HN 仅供人类对话</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47340079' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 4148</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47340079' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1631</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47340079' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New Hacker News guidelines discourage posting comments that are AI-generated or AI-edited. The rule emphasizes preserving authentic human discussion, signal quality, and accountability in community conversations. This matters as AI-assisted writing becomes more common on forums, potentially diluting discourse if not checked.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这条规则强调保持社区讨论的真实人类对话，避免 AI 辅助或编辑的评论影响质感。随着 AI 写作工具日益普及，平台和用户将面临如何维护讨论质量和可追责性的问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/yc500.gif' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/yc500.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47214645' data-tags='Open Source,Security,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47214645' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(2) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://motorolanews.com/motorola-three-new-b2b-solutions-at-mwc-2026/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Motorola announces a partnership with GrapheneOS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>摩托罗拉宣布与 GrapheneOS 基金会达成合作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47214645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 2310</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47214645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 860</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47214645' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Motorola teams up with the GrapheneOS Foundation to focus on security-hardened Android foundations and open-source collaboration. The move could influence future device security updates, transparency, and baseline protections across Motorola hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>摩托罗拉与 GrapheneOS 基金会达成合作，表明在 Android 安全加固与开源协作方面的重点。此举可能影响未来的安全更新、透明度以及硬件基础安全特性的建立。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://motorolanews.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/2024_MOTOROLA_B2B_PHOTOS_150dpi_44-1.png' data-full='https://motorolanews.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/2024_MOTOROLA_B2B_PHOTOS_150dpi_44-1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47584540' data-tags='Security,Open Source,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47584540' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(3) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/Fried_rice/status/2038894956459290963' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Code&#x27;s source code has been leaked via a map file in their NPM registry</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Code 的源代码通过 NPM 注册表中的 map 文件泄露</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47584540' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 2005</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47584540' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 990</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47584540' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code’s source code was leaked through a source map file published in their NPM package registry. The incident underscores how publishing artifacts like source maps can unintentionally expose proprietary code and sensitive logic. It’s a reminder for maintainers to scrub sensitive bits from build artifacts and implement stricter registry hygiene and access controls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code 的源代码通过其 NPM 包注册表中暴露的一个 source map 文件泄露到公网。事件凸显了在发布构建产物时，source map 可能无意间暴露私有代码和业务逻辑的风险。开发团队需要对发布的产物进行清理，并加强注册表的访问控制与审计。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HEubw45WQAA3VRl.jpg:large' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HEubw45WQAA3VRl.jpg:large' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47324054' data-tags='Science,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47324054' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(4) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.computationalcomplexity.org/2026/03/tony-hoare-1934-2026.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tony Hoare has died</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>托尼·霍尔去世</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47324054' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1997</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47324054' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 260</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47324054' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Renowned computer scientist Tony Hoare passed away, leaving a lasting legacy in formal methods, algorithm theory, and programming language design. His work, including Hoare logic and CSP, underpins modern verification, concurrency theory, and software correctness discussions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>著名计算机科学家托尼·霍尔去世，正式方法、算法理论和编程语言设计方面的贡献深远。他提出的霍尔逻辑和 CSP 等奠定了现代形式化验证、并发理论与软件正确性讨论的基石。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blogger.googleusercontent.com/img/b/R29vZ2xl/AVvXsEgvSTdi5rexUms5A0uCuIV72EgjY6hap_Ul1KwUyW1mb-xDB7StvfgP4KLsZySqmf7WyOPQv2qAqZnwUAmmPVQ_XE24g1EGICE7hch44j67cLd2kUc2MwRz87g3Y_uUbOSH9rzULKAYjyKCR4YBZWzTWzZYw1dC53RypJSGWikP5HHZWp0mqcGW/w1200-h630-p-k-no-nu/Jill-and-Tony-Hoare-and-Jim-Miles.jpg' data-full='https://blogger.googleusercontent.com/img/b/R29vZ2xl/AVvXsEgvSTdi5rexUms5A0uCuIV72EgjY6hap_Ul1KwUyW1mb-xDB7StvfgP4KLsZySqmf7WyOPQv2qAqZnwUAmmPVQ_XE24g1EGICE7hch44j67cLd2kUc2MwRz87g3Y_uUbOSH9rzULKAYjyKCR4YBZWzTWzZYw1dC53RypJSGWikP5HHZWp0mqcGW/w1200-h630-p-k-no-nu/Jill-and-Tony-Hoare-and-Jim-Miles.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47247645' data-tags='Hardware,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47247645' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(5) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/03/say-hello-to-macbook-neo/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MacBook Neo</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果推出 MacBook Neo</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47247645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1940</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47247645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2265</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47247645' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple officially launches MacBook Neo, a budget-friendly model powered by A18 Pro, targeting students and light workloads. The device promises improved energy efficiency and a more accessible gateway into Apple&#x27;s ecosystem, potentially expanding its mass-market share.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果正式推出 MacBook Neo，搭载 A18 Pro 芯片，定位入门级，面向学生与轻量级工作。新机承诺提升能效并降低入门门槛，或对苹果在大众市场的份额产生积极影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/03/say-hello-to-macbook-neo/tile/Apple-MacBook-Neo-hero-260304-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202603041429' data-full='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/03/say-hello-to-macbook-neo/tile/Apple-MacBook-Neo-hero-260304-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202603041429' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47582220' data-tags='Security,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47582220' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(6) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.stepsecurity.io/blog/axios-compromised-on-npm-malicious-versions-drop-remote-access-trojan' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Axios compromised on NPM – Malicious versions drop remote access trojan</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Axios 在 NPM 上被劫持：恶意版本投放远程访问木马</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47582220' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1873</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47582220' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 754</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47582220' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Malicious Axios releases popped up on npm, embedding a remote access Trojan in certain versions. The attack highlights the ongoing supply-chain risk for widely used libraries, and operators should audit dependencies, pin safe versions, and monitor for unusual network activity after updates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>npm 上出现恶意的 Axios 版本，包含可对受影响环境进行远程访问的木马。此类供应链攻击再次暴露了对广泛使用库的依赖风险，建议锁定版本、进行依赖审计，并在更新后监测异常网络活动。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/673b71f0790aabf30bd30bf8/69cb2363fdc3f8e8fa0460a5_blog-cover-image.png' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/673b71f0790aabf30bd30bf8/69cb2363fdc3f8e8fa0460a5_blog-cover-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47202708' data-tags='AI,Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47202708' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(7) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://karpathy.github.io/2026/02/12/microgpt/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microgpt</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MicroGPT：轻量级 GPT 的新思路</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47202708' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1847</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47202708' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 309</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47202708' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A compact framework for running GPT-like agents on modest hardware. Emphasizes modular tooling, lightweight memory, and efficient orchestration to enable on-device AI and edge applications. This approach could lower barriers to deploying autonomous AI in real-world products.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出在中等算力设备上运行 GPT 风格代理的紧凑框架。强调模块化工具、轻量级内存和高效编排，以实现边缘设备上的 AI 自主执行。该思路可能降低在真实产品中部署自治 AI 的门槛。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://karpathy.github.io/assets/rssicon.svg' data-full='http://karpathy.github.io/assets/rssicon.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47570269' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47570269' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(8) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://notes.zachmanson.com/copilot-edited-an-ad-into-my-pr/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Copilot edited an ad into my PR</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Copilot 编辑了一条广告进我的 PR</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47570269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1543</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47570269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 629</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47570269' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A firsthand account of Copilot inserting an advertisement into a pull request, illustrating a real risk of AI-assisted edits slipping into code reviews. It underscores the need for guardrails and provenance in AI tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讲述 Copilot 将广告插入到我的 PR 的真实经历，凸显 AI 辅助编辑在代码评审中可能悄然进入的风险。强调对 AI 工具设定护栏与可溯性的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://notes.zachmanson.com/media/copilot-hell.png' data-full='https://notes.zachmanson.com/media/copilot-hell.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47397822' data-tags='Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47397822' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(9) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.timesofisrael.com/gamblers-trying-to-win-a-bet-on-polymarket-are-vowing-to-kill-me-if-i-dont-rewrite-an-iran-missile-story/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Polymarket gamblers threaten to kill me over Iran missile story</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Polymarket 赌徒因伊朗导弹报道威胁要杀我</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47397822' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1537</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47397822' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 976</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47397822' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reporters reveal threats from Polymarket gamblers over an Iran missile story. The threats underscore the real-world risks journalists face when covering volatile bets and geopolitical topics in real-time markets, and raise questions about safety and editorial resilience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>记者报道，有人在 Polymarket 对其伊朗导弹报道下注后威胁要杀害他人。此事凸显在实时博彩市场中报道敏感国际议题时，记者面临的现实风险，以及安全与编辑独立性可能受到的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static-cdn.toi-media.com/www/uploads/2026/03/Polycombo-1.jpg' data-full='https://static-cdn.toi-media.com/www/uploads/2026/03/Polycombo-1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47357042' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47357042' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(10) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gist.github.com/bretonium/291f4388e2de89a43b25c135b44e41f0' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Shall I implement it? No</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>要不要自己实现？不</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47357042' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1518</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47357042' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 553</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47357042' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sometimes the best move is to resist building something from scratch and instead reuse or skip it altogether.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有时最明智的做法不是从零实现某个功能，而是优先复用现成方案或直接放弃该实现。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://github.githubassets.com/assets/gist-og-image-54fd7dc0713e.png' data-full='https://github.githubassets.com/assets/gist-og-image-54fd7dc0713e.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47438723' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47438723' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(11) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://astral.sh/blog/openai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Astral to Join OpenAI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Astral 将加入 OpenAI</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47438723' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1459</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47438723' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 887</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47438723' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Astral will join OpenAI, signaling a consolidation of AI tooling and developer platforms. The move could accelerate integration of Astral&#x27;s capabilities into OpenAI&#x27;s ecosystem, influencing how developers build atop AI services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Astral 将加入 OpenAI，标志着 AI 工具链和开发者平台的整合。这一举措可能加速将 Astral 的能力融入 OpenAI 的生态，影响开发者在 AI 服务上的搭建方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://astral.sh/static/OpenGraph/Astral.jpg' data-full='https://astral.sh/static/OpenGraph/Astral.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47363754' data-tags='AI,Privacy,Security,Open Source,Hardware,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47363754' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(12) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.canirun.ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Can I run AI locally?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>本地能否运行 AI？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47363754' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1435</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47363754' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 343</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47363754' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Running AI locally is increasingly feasible for certain models, but hardware, energy, and cost trade-offs persist against the convenience of cloud inference. The piece surveys current open-source options, performance hurdles, and key privacy and security considerations for offline deployment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在本地运行 AI 对某些模型已逐渐变得可行，但与云端推理相比，仍需权衡硬件、能耗与成本。本文梳理了当前的开源选项、性能瓶颈，以及离线部署的隐私和安全考量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://canirun.ai/og/home.png' data-full='https://canirun.ai/og/home.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47522709' data-tags='Privacy,Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47522709' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(13) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fightchatcontrol.eu/?foo=bar' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The EU still wants to scan  your private messages and photos</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧盟仍欲对私人信息与照片进行扫描</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47522709' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1429</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47522709' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 387</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47522709' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Despite pushback, the EU continues to pursue scanning private messages and photos as part of its safety agenda. The plan raises concerns about encryption, privacy, and the trade-offs between surveillance and individual rights.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟尽管遭到反对，仍在推动对私人信息与照片进行扫描，作为安全与反恐的一部分。该举动引发关于端对端加密、隐私保护与政府监控边界的担忧与争论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://fightchatcontrol.eu/data/logo.webp' data-full='https://fightchatcontrol.eu/data/logo.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47350424' data-tags='Security,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47350424' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(14) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://malus.sh' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Malus – Clean Room as a Service</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Malus：洁净室即服务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47350424' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1400</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47350424' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 519</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47350424' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Malus offers a ‘clean room as a service’ model to support IP-conscious software development without exposing sensitive assets. The approach aims to balance collaboration with IP protection, appealing to teams needing controlled environments for sensitive work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Malus 提供“洁净室即服务”的模式，帮助团队在不暴露敏感资产的前提下进行合规的软件开发。该方法力图在协作与知识产权保护之间取得平衡，适用于需要受控开发环境的场景。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://malus.sh/turd.png' data-full='https://malus.sh/turd.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47225130' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47225130' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(15) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.svd.se/a/K8nrV4/metas-ai-smart-glasses-and-data-privacy-concerns-workers-say-we-see-everything' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta’s AI smart glasses and data privacy concerns</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 的 AI 智能眼镜与数据隐私担忧</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47225130' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1388</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47225130' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 788</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47225130' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta&#x27;s AI glasses spark privacy debates as workers report pervasive sensing and potential data collection. The piece weighs the balance between convenience and surveillance, and what it means for corporate wearables and future AR devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 的 AI 智能眼镜引发隐私争议，员工反映设备在日常使用中存在广泛感知和潜在数据收集。文章讨论便利性与监控之间的权衡，以及这类企业级穿戴设备和未来 AR 设备的治理含义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://svd.vgc.no/v2/images/e4d3cfec-f7dc-4461-aa32-108ea47f2621?h=630&amp;q=80&amp;upscale=true&amp;w=1200&amp;s=d82dc447eb031914967ea232d0aab8207eab5ef0' data-full='https://svd.vgc.no/v2/images/e4d3cfec-f7dc-4461-aa32-108ea47f2621?h=630&amp;q=80&amp;upscale=true&amp;w=1200&amp;s=d82dc447eb031914967ea232d0aab8207eab5ef0' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47408703' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47408703' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(16) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://translate.kagi.com/?from=en&amp;to=LinkedIn+speak' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kagi Translate now supports LinkedIn Speak as an output language</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Kagi Translate 现已支持 LinkedIn Speak 作为输出语言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47408703' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1384</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47408703' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 328</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47408703' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kagi Translate adds LinkedIn Speak as a translation target, enabling outputs in a professional LinkedIn-friendly register. This broadens its channel-aware capabilities, helping users tailor content for business networks and improve engagement on professional posts. It reflects growing demand for channel-specific personas in translation tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kagi Translate 增加了 LinkedIn Speak 作为输出语言，能把翻译结果呈现为专业、适合 LinkedIn 的语气。此举让内容更易在商业网络中引发共鸣，提升职业帖子的互动率。也反映了翻译工具越来越强调按渠道定制的能力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://translate.kagi.com/banner.png' data-full='https://translate.kagi.com/banner.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47189650' data-tags='Security,Politics,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47189650' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(17) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/sama/status/2027578652477821175' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI agrees with Dept. of War to deploy models in their classified network</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 与战争部在其机密网络中部署模型达成协议</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47189650' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1372</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47189650' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 636</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47189650' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI reaches an agreement to deploy its models inside the War Department&#x27;s classified network, marking a rare government-use approval for advanced AI. The deal raises questions about security, vendor oversight, and the scale of AI integration into sensitive environments. It signals a potential path for enterprise and defense collaborations, with tight governance requirements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 达成在战争部机密网络中部署其模型的协议，标志着在高敏环境中对先进 AI 的罕见应用获批。该交易引发对安全、供应商监管以及在敏感场景中部署 AI 的规模等问题的讨论。它预示着企业与防务领域日后更紧密的协作之路，同时将伴随严格的治理要求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1904933748015255552/k43GMz63_200x200.jpg' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1904933748015255552/k43GMz63_200x200.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47556729' data-tags='Health,Startups,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47556729' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(18) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sytse.com/cancer/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Founder of GitLab battles cancer by founding companies</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitLab 创始人通过创办新公司对抗癌症</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47556729' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1369</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47556729' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 247</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47556729' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GitLab&#x27;s cofounder is battling cancer and continues pushing forward by launching new companies. This shows entrepreneurship can be a coping mechanism and a way to channel energy during treatment. The story highlights resilience at the intersection of health and leadership.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GitLab 联合创始人正在与癌症作斗争，但仍通过创办新公司继续前进。这一做法凸显创业在治疗期间用以应对压力的作用，以及在健康挑战中保持生产力的能力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://sytse.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/Screenshot-2026-01-15-at-12.59.23-PM-967x1024.png' data-full='https://sytse.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/Screenshot-2026-01-15-at-12.59.23-PM-967x1024.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47230704' data-tags='Web,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47230704' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(19) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://editor.p5js.org/isohedral/full/vJa5RiZWs' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Xkcd thing, now interactive</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>XKCD 的东西，现在可交互</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47230704' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1252</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47230704' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 157</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47230704' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An interactive rendition of an XKCD concept demonstrates how comics-inspired ideas can translate into interactive web experiences.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>XKCD 的某个知名梗现已实现交互版本，展示了幽默内容与互动界面的结合，以及童趣式的 UI 实验。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47586778' data-tags='Security,Open Source,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47586778' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(20) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://alex000kim.com/posts/2026-03-31-claude-code-source-leak/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Claude Code Source Leak: fake tools, frustration regexes, undercover mode</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude 代码源泄露：假工具、挫败感正则、潜伏模式</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47586778' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1251</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47586778' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 506</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47586778' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An analysis of a Claude code-source leak reveals decoy tools and frustrating regex tests, with mentions of an undercover mode. The incident highlights the fragility of model provenance and the risk of leaked tooling being manipulated to mislead researchers. The takeaway is a call for stronger provenance controls and safer, auditable tooling around AI code-generation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 Claude 代码源泄露事件的分析显示存在伪工具与令人挫败的正则测试，并提及潜伏模式。此事凸显了模型来源的脆弱性，以及泄露工具被操纵以误导研究者的风险。核心启示是需要加强来源追溯与对 AI 代码生成工具的安全、可审计设计。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47507150' data-tags='Gaming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47507150' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.xda-developers.com/wine-11-rewrites-linux-runs-windows-games-speed-gains/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Wine 11 rewrites how Linux runs Windows games at kernel with massive speed gains</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Wine 11：在内核层重写以显著提升 Linux 上运行 Windows 游戏的速度</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47507150' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1246</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47507150' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 469</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47507150' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Wine 11 overhauls kernel interactions to accelerate Windows games on Linux, delivering substantial speedups; it improves compatibility and reduces overhead in system calls translation and graphics handling. This move tightens the gap between Linux gaming and Windows performance, potentially expanding Linux gaming adoption.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Wine 11 对内核层进行重写，使 Linux 上运行 Windows 游戏的性能显著提升，并提高了兼容性与系统调用翻译、图形处理的效率。此举缩小了 Linux 与 Windows 在游戏性能上的差距，有望推动 Linux 游戏生态的扩展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static0.xdaimages.com/wordpress/wp-content/uploads/wm/2025/09/windows-apps-through-wine.jpg?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=crop' data-full='https://static0.xdaimages.com/wordpress/wp-content/uploads/wm/2025/09/windows-apps-through-wine.jpg?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=crop' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47241551' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47241551' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(22) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://grapheneos.social/@GrapheneOS/116160393783585567' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Motorola GrapheneOS devices will be bootloader unlockable/relockable</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>摩托罗拉的 GrapheneOS 设备将支持解锁与重新锁定引导加载程序</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47241551' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1244</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47241551' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 527</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47241551' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Motorola&#x27;s GrapheneOS devices will support bootloader unlock and relock, restoring user control over the device&#x27;s software stack while keeping the security workflow intact. This enables custom ROM flashing and safer recovery paths, aligned with GrapheneOS&#x27;s privacy and hardening goals. It also tests how manufacturers balance openness with enterprise security expectations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>摩托罗拉的 GrapheneOS 设备将支持解锁并重新锁定引导加载程序，重新赋予用户对软件栈的控制，同时保持安全更新流程完备。此举让刷入自定义 ROM 和更安全的设备恢复成为可能，与 GrapheneOS 的隐私与强化安全目标相契合。也反映出厂商在开放性与企业安全之间的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://grapheneos.social/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' data-full='https://grapheneos.social/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47362528' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(23) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/upper-up/meta-lobbying-and-other-findings' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta Platforms: Lobbying, dark money, and the App Store Accountability Act</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 平台：游说、暗钱与 App Store 责任法案</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47362528' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1232</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47362528' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 519</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47362528' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A repository exposes Meta’s lobbying footprint, including hidden political spending and policy influence around app ecosystem accountability. The findings illustrate how big tech’s financial activity intersects with regulatory scrutiny and platform governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个仓库揭示了 Meta 的游说策略、暗钱与围绕应用生态治理的政策影响力。这些发现揭示了大型科技公司在监管审查与平台治理中的资金活动如何交织。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/3e0dd8618e2533360eedf2ab9460f7461a5e103fd14b7cc86456d58fc506bf5e/upper-up/meta-lobbying-and-other-findings' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/3e0dd8618e2533360eedf2ab9460f7461a5e103fd14b7cc86456d58fc506bf5e/upper-up/meta-lobbying-and-other-findings' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47402950' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47402950' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(24) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c70n2x7p22do' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Palestinian boy, 12, describes how Israeli forces killed his family in car</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>12岁巴勒斯坦男孩描述以色列军队在车内杀害家人</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47402950' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1225</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47402950' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 393</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47402950' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 12-year-old Palestinian boy describes how Israeli forces killed his family in a car, offering a rare child’s eyewitness account amid ongoing conflict. The testimony highlights civilian harm and the human cost of military operations, underscoring the volatility of frontline reporting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名12岁巴勒斯坦男孩讲述在一起车内事件中，他的家人被以色列军队杀害的经过。此证词突显冲突中的平民伤亡与人道代价，也反映出前线报道的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/26e0/live/5fb34240-215b-11f1-b297-95b0a0a8331e.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/26e0/live/5fb34240-215b-11f1-b297-95b0a0a8331e.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47460525' data-tags='AI,Open Source,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47460525' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(25) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://opencode.ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenCode – Open source AI coding agent</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenCode：开源AI编码代理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47460525' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1222</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47460525' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 599</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47460525' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenCode is an open-source AI coding agent that helps developers automate routine coding tasks and explore AI-assisted workflows. By being open-source, it invites experimentation, customization, and transparency, contrasting with proprietary copilots. This matters for developers weighing privacy, control, and ecosystem when adopting AI coding tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenCode 是一个开源的 AI 编码代理，旨在帮助开发者自动化常规编码任务并尝试 AI 驱动的工作流。作为开源项目，它鼓励社区参与、定制和透明度，与闭源的代码助手形成对比。对于考虑隐私、控制权和生态系统的开发者来说，这是选择 AI 编码工具时一个重要考量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opencode.ai/social-share.png' data-full='https://opencode.ai/social-share.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47436950' data-tags='Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47436950' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(26) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nypost.com/2026/03/18/us-news/afroman-found-not-liable-in-bizarre-ohio-defamation-case/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Afroman found not liable in defamation case</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Afroman 在诽谤案中被裁定对警察无责</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47436950' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1186</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47436950' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 690</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47436950' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Afroman was found not liable in a defamation suit brought by Ohio police who had raided his home. The ruling emphasizes the high standard for proving defamation when public figures discuss police conduct and may influence future entertainment-law disputes. This outcome matters for creators who voice controversial statements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Afroman 在俄亥俄州警察就突袭其家中提起的诽谤诉讼中被判无责任。判决强调涉及公共人物与执法行为的言论案件需满足较高的证明标准，可能影响未来相关诉讼的走向，并对娱乐行业有一定参考意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nypost.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/03/Comp_-50.jpg?quality=75&amp;strip=all&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://nypost.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/03/Comp_-50.jpg?quality=75&amp;strip=all&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47367129' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47367129' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(27) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://claude.com/blog/1m-context-ga' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>1M context is now generally available for Opus 4.6 and Sonnet 4.6</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Opus 4.6 与 Sonnet 4.6 现已支持 1M 上下文长度</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47367129' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1171</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47367129' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 497</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47367129' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>1M context windows are now generally available for Opus 4.6 and Sonnet 4.6, enabling longer context retention and more coherent long-form assistance. This reduces engineering friction around chunking long documents and improves tasks like document QA, summarization, and multi-turn conversations, though it shifts cost and latency considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Opus 4.6 和 Sonnet 4.6 现推出 1M 上下文长度，便于处理更长的对话和文档。长上下文提升了连续性与可用性，减少对文本分块的依赖，但也带来成本和延迟的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/68a44d4040f98a4adf2207b6/6903d22930b7622d6096c33d_4d663bd87c391c144b9bca513b3849ccfa00a3b9-1000x1000.svg' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/68a44d4040f98a4adf2207b6/6903d22930b7622d6096c33d_4d663bd87c391c144b9bca513b3849ccfa00a3b9-1000x1000.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47442690' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47442690' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(28) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/gadgets/2026/03/google-details-new-24-hour-process-to-sideload-unverified-android-apps/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google details new 24-hour process to sideload unverified Android apps</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌公布 24 小时内可 sideload 未验证 Android 应用的新流程</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47442690' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1159</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47442690' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1237</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47442690' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google detailed a 24-hour window that allows sideloading of unverified Android apps to accelerate testing and internal distribution outside the Play ecosystem. While it speeds iteration, it broadens the attack surface and prompts heightened security and policy safeguards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌公布了一项 24 小时窗口，允许 sideload 未经验证的 Android 应用以加速测试和内部分发。此举虽提升迭代速度，但也扩大了潜在攻击面，需加强安全与策略层面的防护。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/developer-identity-hero-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/developer-identity-hero-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47275088' data-tags='Science,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47275088' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(29) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.researchsquare.com/article/rs-6079807/v1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Global warming has accelerated significantly</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>全球变暖显著加速</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47275088' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1157</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47275088' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1149</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47275088' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New measurements show the pace of global warming has accelerated beyond prior projections, delivering record-high temperatures and more frequent extreme weather events. The finding underscores the urgency for rapid decarbonization and climate-tech innovation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新数据显示全球变暖速率显著加快，突破以往预测，伴随创纪录高温和更频繁的极端天气。该发现凸显了迅速降低碳排放和推动气候科技创新的紧迫性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.researchsquare.com/static/img/logos/logo-rs.svg' data-full='https://www.researchsquare.com/static/img/logos/logo-rs.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47223620' data-tags='Politics,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47223620' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(30) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cbc.ca/news/canada/british-columbia/b-c-adopting-year-round-daylight-time-9.7111657' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>British Columbia is permanently adopting daylight time</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不列颠哥伦比亚省永久采用夏令时</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47223620' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1140</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47223620' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 553</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47223620' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>British Columbia will permanently observe daylight time, ending seasonal clock changes. The move aims to simplify scheduling and energy planning, but could complicate activities tied to daylight patterns and cross-border coordination with the US and other provinces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>不列颠哥伦比亚省将永久采用夏令时，取消季节性时钟调整。此举旨在简化日程与能源规划，但可能让依赖日照模式的活动和跨境协调变得更复杂。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47191232' data-tags='Politics,Security,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47191232' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(31) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnn.com/2026/02/28/middleeast/israel-attack-iran-intl-hnk' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The United States and Israel have launched a major attack on Iran</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美以对伊朗发动大规模攻击</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47191232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1136</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47191232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2477</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47191232' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The US and Israel have launched a major strike on Iran, signaling a significant geopolitical shift with potential ripple effects on energy markets, cyber operations, and regional tech policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美以对伊朗发动大规模军事行动，可能对能源市场、网络行动与区域技术政策产生连锁影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.cnn.com/api/v1/images/stellar/prod/ap26059256811672.jpg?c=16x9&amp;q=w_800,c_fill' data-full='https://media.cnn.com/api/v1/images/stellar/prod/ap26059256811672.jpg?c=16x9&amp;q=w_800,c_fill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47216047' data-tags='Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47216047' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(32) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.windowslatest.com/2026/03/02/microsoft-gets-tired-of-microslop-bans-the-word-on-its-discord-then-locks-the-server-after-backlash/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>“Microslop” filtered in the official Microsoft Copilot Discord server</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>官方 Copilot Discord 服务器屏蔽“Microslop”一词</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47216047' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1124</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47216047' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 514</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft blocks the term &#x27;Microslop&#x27; in its Copilot Discord server, then locks the channel after backlash. The incident underscores tensions between branding, community moderation, and how corporate AI products are managed in public spaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软在官方 Copilot Discord 服务器屏蔽了“Microslop”一词，遭遇反对后又封锁服务器。此事件凸显品牌控制、社区治理以及在公开场合管理企业 AI 产品的张力。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47517539' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Slowing down can protect long-term quality and prevent burnout in fast-moving tech work. The piece argues for deliberate pacing, better boundaries, and smarter prioritization to sustain creativity and reliability over time.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>放慢节奏或许比盲目追求速度更能保障长期产出质量，避免职业倦怠。文中倡导有意识的节奏控制、设定边界以及更聪明的优先级排序，以维持创造力和可靠性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(35) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wsj.com/politics/policy/judge-orders-government-to-begin-refunding-more-than-130-billion-in-tariffs-fdc1e62c' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Judge orders government to begin refunding more than $130B in tariffs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法官命令政府开始退还超过1300亿美元的关税</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47261688' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1056</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47261688' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 762</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47261688' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A judge ordered the government to begin refunding more than $130 billion in tariffs collected over the years, a move that could ease costs for importers and reshape trade dynamics. The ruling may accelerate how swiftly refunds are processed and influence ongoing tariff policy debates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法官命令政府开始退还累计超过1300亿美元的关税。这笔退款将减轻进口商成本，并可能重塑关税政策的执行节奏与相关讨论。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47263323' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47263323' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(36) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wikimediastatus.net' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Wikipedia was in read-only mode following mass admin account compromise</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>维基百科因大规模管理员账户被入侵而进入只读模式</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47263323' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1038</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47263323' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 376</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47263323' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mass admin account compromise forced Wikipedia into read-only mode to protect content integrity. The incident underscores the risk of credential breaches in high-privilege accounts and the need for rapid incident response, MFA enforcement, and auditing for large collaborative platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>大量管理员账户被入侵，迫使维基百科进入只读模式以防止进一步编辑。此事件凸显高权限账户安全风险，以及快速处置、强制多因素认证和审计的重要性，尤其对大型协作型百科平台。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dka575ofm4ao0.cloudfront.net/pages-transactional_logos/retina/233622/2560px-Wikimedia_Foundation_logo_-_horizontal.svg.png' data-full='https://dka575ofm4ao0.cloudfront.net/pages-transactional_logos/retina/233622/2560px-Wikimedia_Foundation_logo_-_horizontal.svg.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47500335' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(37) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sambent.com/microsofts-plan-to-fix-windows-11-is-gaslighting/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft&#x27;s &quot;fix&quot; for Windows 11</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软对 Windows 11 的“修复”：挨打后的花束</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47500335' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1023</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47500335' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 738</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47500335' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft continues to roll out patches for Windows 11, but critics argue the changes are cosmetic and don&#x27;t address core reliability or usability issues. The messaging resembles a post-incident PR sprint rather than substantive engineering fixes. For tech readers, this highlights the gap between marketing narratives and real-world OS quality in major updates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软继续推出 Windows 11 的补丁，但批评者认为这些改动多为表面修补，无法解决核心的可靠性或可用性问题。外部宣传更像是事后公关冲刺，而非实质工程改进。对技术读者而言，这凸显了大型操作系统更新中营销叙述与实际软件质量之间的差距。</p>

It spotlights the human dimension of AI-assisted work and the value of reusable, long‑term motivation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位60岁程序员分享，Claude Code 重燃了他对编程的热情，体现 AI 辅助工具在激励老练开发者方面的潜力。也提醒我们，AI 助手的价值在于激发人们对长期学习与创作的热情。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47518960' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47518960' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(39) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bethmathews.substack.com/p/why-so-many-control-rooms-were-seafoam' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why so many control rooms were seafoam green (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么大量控制室偏好海泡绿（2025）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47518960' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1006</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47518960' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 200</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47518960' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The color choice in control rooms often favors seafoam green because it reduces glare and supports sustained focus during long shifts. The piece ties this preference to ergonomics, display tech, and a broader design culture that values calm, legibility, and efficiency in high-stakes environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>控制室的颜色选择常常偏向海泡绿，因为它能降低眩光、帮助在长时间工作中保持专注。本文将这一偏好与人体工效学、显示技术以及强调冷静、易读与高效的设计文化联系起来，探讨在高压环境中的实用性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(40) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/JosephPolitano/status/2029916364664611242' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tech employment now significantly worse than the 2008 or 2020 recessions</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47278426' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1002</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47278426' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 677</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47278426' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tech labor market deteriorated more sharply than during the 2008 financial crisis or the 2020 recession, with hiring slowdowns and rising layoffs across the sector. The trend raises questions about demand for tech talent, AI adoption, and policy responses.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>科技行业的就业市场比2008年金融危机或2020年衰退期间更为疲弱，出现招聘放缓和裁员增加。这一趋势引发对技术需求、AI应用和政策应对的担忧。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47307055' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pv-magazine.com/2025/06/20/ireland-coal-free-ends-coal-power-generation-moneypoint/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ireland shuts last coal plant, becomes 15th coal-free country in Europe (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>爱尔兰关闭最后一座煤电厂，成为欧洲第15个无煤国家（2025）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47307055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 996</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47307055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 626</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47307055' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ireland has closed its last coal-fired plant, reaching coal-free status and marking a milestone for Europe’s energy transition. The move accelerates renewables adoption and grid resilience challenges; it may influence Europe’s energy market and investment in storage and interconnectors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>爱尔兰关闭最后一座煤电厂，正式实现全境无煤化，成为欧洲第15个无煤国家。此举加速可再生能源占比提升，同时对电网稳定、储能建设与区域互联提出新挑战与机遇。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.pv-magazine.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/06/ESB_Moneypoint_Generating_Station_-_geograph.org_.uk_-_12606_crop.v1.jpg' data-full='https://www.pv-magazine.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/06/ESB_Moneypoint_Generating_Station_-_geograph.org_.uk_-_12606_crop.v1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47392084' data-tags='Privacy,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47392084' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(43) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.michaelgeist.ca/2026/03/a-tale-of-two-bills-lawful-access-returns-with-changes-to-warrantless-access-but-dangerous-backdoor-surveillance-risks-remains/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bill C-22, the Lawful Access Act: Dangerous backdoor surveillance risks remain</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Bill C-22：合法获取法案——后门监控风险仍存</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47392084' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 988</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47392084' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 328</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47392084' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bill C-22 would expand law enforcement access to data, with changes that still allow warrantless backdoor surveillance risks. Privacy advocates warn that even with reforms, the bill could enable broad, covert monitoring across digital communications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bill C-22 将扩大执法对数据的获取权限，尽管作出了一些修改，后门监控风险仍然存在。隐私倡导者警告这类立法可能让大范围、非授权的监控成为现实，侵害公民数字隐私。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.michaelgeist.ca/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/screenshot_4102.png' data-full='https://www.michaelgeist.ca/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/screenshot_4102.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47454341' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47454341' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(44) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/2026/03/im-ok-being-left-behind-thanks/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I&#x27;m OK being left behind, thanks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谢谢，我愿意落后一点</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47454341' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 968</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47454341' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 751</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47454341' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Many readers may interpret as a stance against the AI arms race. It argues for a slower, more deliberate pace focused on reliability and user welfare. In practice, it challenges the assumption that faster is always better in tech development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者不愿被技术浪潮淹没，选择慢一些、稳妥前进。强调稳定性和隐私比追逐热点更重要。这样的观点提醒在快速迭代的行业里，理性节奏同样值得关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/wp-content/uploads/2022/08/Smile-Metaverse.webp' data-full='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/wp-content/uploads/2022/08/Smile-Metaverse.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47197505' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47197505' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(45) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://garymarcus.substack.com/p/the-whole-thing-was-scam' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The whole thing was a scam</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>整個事情都是詐騙</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47197505' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 967</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47197505' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 309</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47197505' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gary Marcus argues that a celebrated AI initiative or narrative was built on hype and misrepresentations. The piece calls out inflated claims, flawed methods, and incentives that rewarded hype over real engineering progress. It serves as a cautionary reminder to scrutinize AI marketing versus tangible engineering advances.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gary Marcus 指出某個知名 AI 計畫或敘事建立在炒作與誤導之上，揭露夸大聲稱與方法論缺陷，以及把炒作利益置於真正工程進展之上的動機。文章提醒讀者要區辨 AI 行銷與實際工程成就，避免被包裝過的繁榮所誤導。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47232768' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47232768' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(46) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://neilzone.co.uk/2026/03/im-struggling-to-think-of-any-online-services-for-which-id-be-willing-to-verify-my-identity-or-age/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I&#x27;m reluctant to verify my identity or age for any online services</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我不愿意在任何在线服务上验证身份或年龄</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47232768' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 964</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47232768' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 613</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47232768' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Verifying identity or age has become a friction point in many online services, and I’m reluctant to do it. The post argues that invasive KYC checks erode privacy and push users away, and it hints at privacy-preserving alternatives and the broader trend toward minimal verification.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在线服务对身份/年龄的验证日益增多，造成用户体验负担。该文指出强制性的身份核验侵蚀隐私、抑制使用意愿，并探讨更隐私友好的身份认证替代方案以及数字身份的未来趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://neilzone.co.uk/content/images/2024-10-17_neil.jpg' data-full='https://neilzone.co.uk/content/images/2024-10-17_neil.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47523330' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47523330' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(47) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bugs.xdavidhu.me/tesla/2026/03/23/running-tesla-model-3s-computer-on-my-desk-using-parts-from-crashed-cars/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Running Tesla Model 3&#x27;s computer on my desk using parts from crashed cars</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用桌面裝置與回收車輛零件運行特斯拉 Model 3 的電腦</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47523330' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 961</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47523330' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 328</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47523330' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A maker demonstrates powering and running the Tesla Model 3&#x27;s computer on a desktop rig using salvaged parts from crashed cars. The project shows automotive-grade compute can be repurposed for off-car experimentation, highlighting both learning value and safety/compatibility challenges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位硬件爱好者用从撞车事故中回收的部件，在桌面上搭建并运行特斯拉 Model 3 的计算机。该项目展示了汽车级算力可以被改作桌面实验，但也暴露出安全与兼容性方面的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bugs.xdavidhu.me/assets/posts/2026-03-23-running-tesla-model-3s-computer-on-my-desk-using-parts-from-crashed-cars/card.jpg' data-full='https://bugs.xdavidhu.me/assets/posts/2026-03-23-running-tesla-model-3s-computer-on-my-desk-using-parts-from-crashed-cars/card.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47359744' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(48) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://samhenri.gold/blog/20260312-this-is-not-the-computer-for-you/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>“This is not the computer for you”</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>这不是适合你的那台电脑</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47359744' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 959</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47359744' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 369</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47359744' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece argues that a given computer isn&#x27;t the right tool for everyone, challenging the idea that one device fits all needs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文质疑某台电脑并非适合所有人，挑战“一个设备适合所有需求”的观点。</p>
