<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47566865' data-tags='Security,AI,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47566865' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(49) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.buchodi.com/chatgpt-wont-let-you-type-until-cloudflare-reads-your-react-state-i-decrypted-the-program-that-does-it/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ChatGPT won&#x27;t let you type until Cloudflare reads your React state</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ChatGPT 在 Cloudflare 读取你的 React 状态前不让你输入</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47566865' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 957</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47566865' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 612</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47566865' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Some anti-bot protections gate user input until the browser reveals internal React state, illustrating how client-side signals shape UX.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一些反爬虫防护在浏览器暴露内部 React 状态前就阻止输入，揭示了客户端信号如何影响用户体验。此类防护引发对隐私、性能以及基于 JavaScript 的检测策略韧性的讨论。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47423647' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47423647' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(50) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cs.unc.edu/~stotts/COMP590-059-f24/robsrules.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rob Pike’s Rules of Programming (1989)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rob Pike 的五条编程法则</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47423647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 947</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47423647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 441</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47423647' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Practical guidance from Rob Pike emphasizes keeping code simple, correct, and maintainable. The five rules center on readable interfaces, orthogonality, and constructing reliable, easily reasoned systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rob Pike 的五条编程法则集中呈现了实用的编程智慧，强调构建可靠软件的重要性。核心内容包括简洁、可读接口、正交性，以及易于推理和维护的代码。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47255881' data-tags='Programming,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47255881' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(51) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/googleworkspace/cli' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google Workspace CLI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Google Workspace 命令行工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47255881' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 916</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47255881' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 285</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47255881' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Google Workspace CLI project provides a command-line interface for automating and scripting Workspace admin tasks. It enables developers to manage users, drive configurations, and integrate with existing CI/CD workflows. The move aligns with a broader push to make cloud admin workflows more programmable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Google Workspace CLI 提供了用于自动化和脚本化管理任务的命令行界面，方便开发者对用户、配置等执行操作并集成到 CI/CD 流水线。此举体现云端管理工作流向可编程化的趋势，提升运维效率。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/af59fd92876ec3ef02c4e4e131420cb93768ef44740516fdf45ea060089a4636/googleworkspace/cli' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/af59fd92876ec3ef02c4e4e131420cb93768ef44740516fdf45ea060089a4636/googleworkspace/cli' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47501426' data-tags='Security,AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47501426' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(52) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/BerriAI/litellm/issues/24512' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tell HN: Litellm 1.82.7 and 1.82.8 on PyPI are compromised</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Litellm 1.82.7 与 1.82.8 在 PyPI 上遭篡改</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47501426' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 909</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47501426' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 479</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47501426' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Two releases of a lightweight LLM client package were compromised on PyPI, enabling malicious code pushes to downstream users. The incident underscores supply-chain risk in Python ecosystems and the importance of checksum validation and pinning versions. Users should audit dependencies, upgrade to clean builds, and verify package integrity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>两个版本的轻量级 LLM 客户端包在 PyPI 的发布被篡改，导致后续用户可能被注入恶意代码。事件凸显 Python 生态的供应链风险，强调校验和版本锁定的重要性。请审计依赖、升级到干净的构建版本，并验证包的完整性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/879c9c5c8544440c1f91210a5c60ea3b63b4bb81e93c0b1239ff87aab7717395/BerriAI/litellm/issues/24512' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/879c9c5c8544440c1f91210a5c60ea3b63b4bb81e93c0b1239ff87aab7717395/BerriAI/litellm/issues/24512' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47487436' data-tags='Privacy,Data,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47487436' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(53) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rz01.org/eu-migration/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Migrating to the EU</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>迁移到欧盟</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47487436' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 900</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47487436' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 691</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47487436' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Relocating workloads to EU infrastructure helps meet data-residency and privacy requirements under GDPR-era norms. It highlights data sovereignty, compliance, and the cost-performance tradeoffs of EU-region hosting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将工作负载迁移到欧盟基础设施有助于符合数据驻留和隐私规定。讨论数据主权、合规性，以及在欧盟区域托管的成本与性能权衡。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47477873' data-tags='Entertainment,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47477873' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(54) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rpastro.square.site/s/stories/phm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>My astrophotography in the movie Project Hail Mary</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我在电影《Project Hail Mary》中的天体摄影</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47477873' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 892</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47477873' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 199</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47477873' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece recounts the author&#x27;s astrophotography work for the film Project Hail Mary, detailing the gear, techniques, and how practical photography informs on-screen space imagery. It also highlights how real astronomy influences cinematic visuals.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者在电影《Project Hail Mary》中进行天体摄影的亲身经历，介绍所用器材、曝光策略，以及真实天文影像与特效的衔接。揭示了天体摄影与影视叙事之间的结合。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://rpastro.square.site/uploads/b/b26eba30-e7ea-11ee-8dc4-a9472e181ebe/IMG_0058.PNG' data-full='https://rpastro.square.site/uploads/b/b26eba30-e7ea-11ee-8dc4-a9472e181ebe/IMG_0058.PNG' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47534848' data-tags='Politics,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47534848' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(55) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.derekthompson.org/p/we-havent-seen-the-worst-of-what' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We haven&#x27;t seen the worst of what gambling and prediction markets will do</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们还没看到赌博与预测市场的最坏后果</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47534848' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 891</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47534848' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 683</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47534848' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that gambling and prediction markets will magnify risks as AI makes them scalable and accessible. It discusses potential manipulation, bias, and regulatory challenges, urging careful design and governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为，随着 AI 提升预测市场的可扩展性和可访问性，赌博与预测市场的风险还会进一步放大。讨论潜在的操纵、偏见和监管挑战，呼吁在设计与治理上保持谨慎。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!r41H!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fd037a2b3-283d-4ef2-92da-b1b95e22e446_1080x683.jpeg' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!r41H!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fd037a2b3-283d-4ef2-92da-b1b95e22e446_1080x683.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47421442' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47421442' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(56) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.otherstrangeness.com/2026/03/14/have-a-fucking-website/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Have a fucking website</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一定要有一个网站</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47421442' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 889</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47421442' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 505</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47421442' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Having a dedicated website gives you a stable home for your projects, portfolio, and experiments, independent of platform policies. A minimal static site with a custom domain can dramatically improve your online credibility and discoverability for developers and creators.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>拥有专属网站为项目、简历与实验成果提供稳定归宿，不依赖社交媒体平台。即使是简单的静态站点搭配自定义域名，也能显著提升可发现性与专业形象。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.otherstrangeness.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/someone-please-tell-me-where-this-image-comes-from-v0-on5002nlkxyd1.jpeg-e1773478577120.webp' data-full='https://www.otherstrangeness.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/someone-please-tell-me-where-this-image-comes-from-v0-on5002nlkxyd1.jpeg-e1773478577120.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47587935' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47587935' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rollingout.com/2026/03/31/oracle-slashes-30000-jobs-with-a-cold-6/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Oracle slashes 30k jobs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Oracle裁员3万</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47587935' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 880</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47587935' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 801</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47587935' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Oracle is cutting 30,000 jobs as part of a broad cost-cutting and restructuring effort amid cloud and ERP market shifts. The move underscores how big software vendors are recalibrating headcount to focus on core cloud platforms, with ripple effects for tech jobs, supplier ecosystems, and investor sentiment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>甲骨文宣布裁减3万名员工，作为成本削减和云转型的一部分。此举凸显大型软件厂商在云平台优先级调整对技术工作、生态体系与投资者情绪的潜在影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://rollingout.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/shutterstock_2351343609-scaled-e1773175981701.jpg' data-full='https://rollingout.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/shutterstock_2351343609-scaled-e1773175981701.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47252971' data-tags='Hardware,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47252971' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(58) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mas.to/@gabrielesvelto/116171750653898304' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>10% of Firefox crashes are caused by bitflips</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Firefox 崩溃中有 10% 由位翻转引发</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47252971' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 875</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47252971' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 458</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47252971' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports that about 10% of Firefox crashes are caused by bitflips in memory, underscoring the impact of hardware faults on software reliability. The finding motivates better error handling, ECC memory use, and robust testing for resilience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道显示约 10% 的 Firefox 崩溃由内存位翻转引起，凸显硬件故障对软件可靠性的影响。该发现促使加强错误处理、使用 ECC 内存以及提高容错测试。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mas.to/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' data-full='https://mas.to/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47410870' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47410870' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(59) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.gadgetreview.com/reddit-user-uncovers-who-is-behind-metas-2b-lobbying-for-invasive-age-verification-tech' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Reddit User Uncovers Who Is Behind Meta&#x27;s $2B Lobbying for Age Verification Tech</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Reddit 用户揭露谁在为 Meta 的年龄验证技术投入 20 亿美元进行游说</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47410870' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 871</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47410870' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47410870' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Reddit user exposes who is behind Meta’s $2 billion lobbying push for age verification tech. The reveal sheds light on the opaque networks that drive big-tech policy influence and raises questions about privacy, consent, and the effectiveness of age checks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位 Reddit 用户披露了推动 Meta 就年龄验证技术进行20亿美元游说的幕后主体。这一揭露揭示了驱动大型科技公司政策影响的隐蔽网络，并引发关于隐私、同意和年龄核验有效性的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.gadgetreview.com/wp-content/uploads/38-artapixel-meta-logo-icon-3d-social-media-scaled.jpg' data-full='https://www.gadgetreview.com/wp-content/uploads/38-artapixel-meta-logo-icon-3d-social-media-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47246110' data-tags='Career,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47246110' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(60) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://terriblesoftware.org/2026/03/03/nobody-gets-promoted-for-simplicity/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nobody gets promoted for simplicity</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>没人因为简单而升职</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47246110' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 852</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47246110' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 490</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47246110' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An opinion piece argues that promotions in many tech organizations reward complexity and visibility rather than keeping systems simple. The author points to entrenched incentives that favor adding features, optimization, or risk-taking over clean, minimal design. The takeaway is to prioritize clarity, reliability, and lean architectures to unlock long-term value and career advancement.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇观点文章认为，许多科技组织的晋升机制偏向复杂性和可见度，而非坚持简单设计。作者指出习以成年的激励让人们更重视增添功能、优化和冒险，而不是追求清晰、稳定的系统。核心信息是，长远价值来自清晰、可靠、简化的架构，有助于职业发展的提升。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/terriblesoftware.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/chjpdmf0zs9sci9pbwfnzxmvd2vic2l0zs8ymdiylta1l25zmtexndetaw1hz2uta3d2d3b1c3kuanbn.webp?fit=1024%2C680&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/terriblesoftware.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/chjpdmf0zs9sci9pbwfnzxmvd2vic2l0zs8ymdiylta1l25zmtexndetaw1hz2uta3d2d3b1c3kuanbn.webp?fit=1024%2C680&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47522173' data-tags='Education,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47522173' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(61) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://whoami.wiki/blog/personal-encyclopedias' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Personal Encyclopedias</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>个人百科全书</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47522173' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 850</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47522173' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 179</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47522173' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores how individuals build personal encyclopedias to organize knowledge, notes, and references into a portable, searchable knowledge base. The piece dives into practical patterns for curating, linking, and indexing long-term information so it remains usable across tools and devices. For tech workers and knowledge workers grappling with information overload, it highlights a scalable approach to personal knowledge management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨个人如何建立百科式知识库，将笔记、引用和资料整合成可检索的本地知识体系。文章深入讨论对条目进行整理、链接与索引的实用模式，以便在不同工具和设备间保持可用性。对于面临信息过载的技术工作者和知识工作者，这种个人知识管理的可扩展方法尤为重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://whoami.wiki/blog/personal-encyclopedias/opengraph-image.png?v=2' data-full='https://whoami.wiki/blog/personal-encyclopedias/opengraph-image.png?v=2' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47232453' data-tags='Hardware,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47232453' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(62) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/03/apple-introduces-macbook-pro-with-all-new-m5-pro-and-m5-max/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MacBook Pro with M5 Pro and M5 Max</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>搭载全新 M5 Pro 与 M5 Max 的 MacBook Pro</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47232453' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 849</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47232453' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 939</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47232453' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple introduces the MacBook Pro with the new M5 Pro and M5 Max chips, signaling another generational leap in performance and efficiency. Pro workflows like 3D rendering, video editing, and ML workloads should see meaningful speedups, with improved energy efficiency and media acceleration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果推出搭载全新 M5 Pro/Max 的 MacBook Pro，预示性能与能效进入新一代。3D 渲染、视频编辑和机器学习等专业工作流有望显著提速，同时提升媒体处理能力与续航表现。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/03/apple-introduces-macbook-pro-with-all-new-m5-pro-and-m5-max/tile/Apple-MacBook-Pro-M5-Pro-and-M5-Max-Capture-One-260303-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202603031721' data-full='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/03/apple-introduces-macbook-pro-with-all-new-m5-pro-and-m5-max/tile/Apple-MacBook-Pro-M5-Pro-and-M5-Max-Capture-One-260303-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202603031721' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47206009' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47206009' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(63) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ghostty.org/docs' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ghostty – Terminal Emulator</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ghostty 终端模拟器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47206009' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 846</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47206009' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 355</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47206009' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ghostty is a modern terminal emulator project aimed at boosting UX with features like multi-tab support and performance improvements. For power users, a refined terminal experience can significantly boost productivity across desktop and mobile workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ghostty 是一个面向提升用户体验的现代终端模拟器项目，可能包含多标签等功能和性能改进。对资深开发者而言，优化的终端体验能显著提升桌面与移动工作流程的生产力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ghostty.org/social-share-card.jpg' data-full='https://ghostty.org/social-share-card.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47582043' data-tags='Science,Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47582043' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(64) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://idlewords.com/2026/03/artemis_ii_is_not_safe_to_fly.htm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Artemis II is not safe to fly</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Artemis II 不宜飞行：存在安全隐患</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47582043' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 843</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47582043' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 584</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47582043' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Artemis II safety concerns argue the crewed lunar flyby is riskier than desired given current data, urging more analysis, testing, and risk mitigation before launch.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>阿耳忒弥斯II 的安全性引发质疑，认为在当前数据与测试水平下执行载人月球飞行风险较高，需要进一步分析、测试和风险缓解。文章强调推进大胆太空任务时安全评估的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://idlewords.com/images/toothfish.png' data-full='https://idlewords.com/images/toothfish.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47480507' data-tags='Culture,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47480507' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(65) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stuartbreckenridge.net/2026-03-19-pc-gamer-recommends-rss-readers-in-a-37mb-article/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PC Gamer recommends RSS readers in a 37mb article that just keeps downloading</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PC Gamer 在一篇 37MB 的文章中推荐 RSS 阅读器，但文稿似乎不断在下载</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47480507' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 835</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47480507' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 392</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47480507' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PC Gamer curates a selection of RSS readers in a long, 37MB feature. The piece itself is unexpectedly download-heavy, turning a simple recommendation into a test of browser patience. The underlying takeaway is that enthusiasts still crave lightweight, dependable feed readers in an era of endless content.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PC Gamer 选出一批 RSS 阅读器的清单，但这篇 37MB 的文章本身却像在持续下载内容，考验浏览器耐心。文章透露在信息过载的时代，用户仍然需要轻量、可靠的订阅工具。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://stuartbreckenridge.net/content/images/2026/03/pc-gamer-1.png' data-full='https://stuartbreckenridge.net/content/images/2026/03/pc-gamer-1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47270784' data-tags='Privacy,Politics,Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47270784' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.system76.com/post/system76-on-age-verification/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>System76 on Age Verification Laws</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>System76 对年龄验证法的看法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47270784' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 825</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47270784' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 581</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47270784' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>System76 argues that age-verification requirements risk privacy and accessibility for users and developers, potentially burdening open-source hardware and software ecosystems. The post calls for balanced policy that protects vulnerable users without stifling innovation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>System76 指出年龄验证要求可能侵犯隐私、降低可访问性，并给开源硬件和软件生态带来额外负担。文中呼吁在保护脆弱用户与不扼杀创新之间实现政策平衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.system76.com/images/social.jpg' data-full='https://blog.system76.com/images/social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47467537' data-tags='Career'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47467537' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(67) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lucumr.pocoo.org/2026/3/20/some-things-just-take-time/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Some things just take time</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>有些事就是需要时间</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47467537' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 825</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47467537' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 268</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47467537' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Patience is essential for long-running tech work; complex problems don&#x27;t yield quick wins. The piece argues that taking time to iterate and learn is often the difference between failure and breakthrough.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在长期的技术工作中，耐心至关重要；复杂问题难以快速解决。文章指出，慢慢迭代和积累经验往往比急于求成带来更大成功的可能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lucumr.pocoo.org/social/2026-03-20-some-things-just-take-time-social.png' data-full='https://lucumr.pocoo.org/social/2026-03-20-some-things-just-take-time-social.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47470991' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47470991' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(68) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.dyne.org/child-protection-is-not-access-control/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Do Not Turn Child Protection into Internet Access Control</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不要把儿童保护变成互联网访问控制</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47470991' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 821</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47470991' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 433</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47470991' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Guardrails for child protection should be precise and targeted, not a pretext for blanket internet access control. Overly broad censorship risks chilling freedom online while failing to stop abuse. The piece calls for privacy-preserving, surgical safeguards instead.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>儿童保护政策不应成为全面的网络访问控制的借口。宽泛的封锁容易压制在线自由，并未有效阻止滥用。应采用更精准、透明的隐私保护措施与安全防护。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.dyne.org/content/images/size/w1200/2026/03/90d2be8e-f920-4ac4-9c8a-092bc2ce88bf.png' data-full='https://news.dyne.org/content/images/size/w1200/2026/03/90d2be8e-f920-4ac4-9c8a-092bc2ce88bf.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47381791' data-tags='Open Source,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47381791' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(69) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://agelesslinux.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ageless Linux – Software for humans of indeterminate age</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ageless Linux：面向各年龄段的 Linux 系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47381791' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 815</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47381791' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 608</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47381791' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ageless Linux aims to be usable by people across a wide range of ages, focusing on human-centered design and accessibility. It challenges the stereotype that Linux is only for coders by providing a gentler, more inclusive user experience and documentation. The project highlights a trend toward more approachable, age-inclusive open-source software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ageless Linux 以以人为本的设计为核心，致力于让不同年龄段的用户都能轻松使用 Linux，并提供友好的界面与文档。此举打破了“Linux 只给程序员用”的刻板印象，体现出开源软件在可访问性与包容性方面的进步。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47200420' data-tags='Business,Legal,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47200420' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(70) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/OpenAI/status/2027846016423321831' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We do not think Anthropic should be designated as a supply chain risk</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我們認為 Anthropic 不應被視為供應鏈風險</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47200420' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 807</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47200420' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 435</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47200420' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI clarifies that Anthropic should not be treated as a special supply-chain risk, arguing for standard vendor diligence instead. This stance underscores how businesses weigh safety concerns against collaboration when sourcing AI services. It reflects the ongoing debate over risk labeling in enterprise AI sourcing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 明確表示 Anthropic 不應被視為特殊的供應鏈風險，主張以常規供應商盡職調查來評估風險。此立場顯示企業在採購 AI 服務時如何在安全與協作之間取得平衡，亦反映關於風險分類的持續爭論。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1885410181409820672/ztsaR0JW_200x200.jpg' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1885410181409820672/ztsaR0JW_200x200.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47301085' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47301085' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(71) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://agent-safehouse.dev/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Agent Safehouse – macOS-native sandboxing for local agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Agent Safehouse：macOS 原生本地代理沙箱</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47301085' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 798</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47301085' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 177</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47301085' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Agent Safehouse provides macOS-native sandboxing for local agents, letting autonomous tasks run in isolated environments without container overhead. By evading full containers while maintaining isolation, it reduces cross-agent risk and strengthens security for macOS automation workflows. This approach matters for developers deploying agents on Mac workstations or in Mac-centric stacks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Agent Safehouse 提供了 macOS 原生的本地代理沙箱化，允许自主任务在相对独立的环境中运行而无需容器开销。通过在不牺牲隔离的前提下提高安全性，它降低了跨代理的风险，提升了 macOS 自动化工作流的安全性。对于在 Mac 环境中部署代理的开发者尤为相关。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://agent-safehouse.dev/og-image.png' data-full='https://agent-safehouse.dev/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47433058' data-tags='Politics,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47433058' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(72) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pew.org/en/research-and-analysis/articles/2026/03/18/austins-surge-of-new-housing-construction-drove-down-rents' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Austin’s surge of new housing construction drove down rents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>奥斯汀新建住房激增致租金下降</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47433058' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 793</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47433058' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 973</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47433058' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Austin’s housing boom kept rents in check as a wave of new units came online, softening affordability even as demand from tech workers remains high. The finding highlights how supplying more units can influence urban affordability and inform policy debates on zoning and infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>奥斯汀的住房供给激增带动新建单位上线，抵消了需求，导致租金走低。此现象反映出供给侧扩张对城市可负担性的直接影响，可能影响土地使用、基础设施和住房政策的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.pew.org/-/media/post-launch-images/2026/03/gettyimages1446174163jpgmaster/16x9_m.jpg' data-full='https://www.pew.org/-/media/post-launch-images/2026/03/gettyimages1446174163jpgmaster/16x9_m.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47390945' data-tags='Web,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47390945' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(73) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thatshubham.com/blog/news-audit' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The 49MB web page</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>49MB 的网页</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47390945' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 792</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47390945' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 353</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47390945' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The page weighs in at 49MB, serving as a cautionary example of how modern sites can balloon with images, scripts, and third-party resources. The write-up walks through auditing approaches to identify bloated bundles, optimize critical paths, and pare back assets without wrecking UX. Takeaway: page size is a first-class performance lever, and thoughtful auditing matters for both users and developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该网页达到 49MB，揭示现代站点如何被图片、脚本和第三方资源膨胀。文中提供审计方法，帮助识别冗余资源、优化关键渲染路径，并在不破坏用户体验的前提下精简资产。要点：页面大小是性能的重要变量，细致的审计对用户和开发者都重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://thatshubham.com/img/news-audit-og.jpg' data-full='https://thatshubham.com/img/news-audit-og.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47413876' data-tags='Security,Gaming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47413876' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(74) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/video-games/console-gaming/microsofts-unhackable-xbox-one-has-been-hacked-by-bliss-the-2013-console-finally-fell-to-voltage-glitching-allowing-the-loading-of-unsigned-code-at-every-level' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft&#x27;s &#x27;unhackable&#x27; Xbox One has been hacked by &#x27;Bliss&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软所谓的“不可破解”的 Xbox One 被 Bliss 破解</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47413876' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 789</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47413876' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 291</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47413876' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers demonstrated a voltage-glitch attack against Microsoft&#x27;s Xbox One, breaking the console&#x27;s claimed &#x27;unhackable&#x27; security. The hack lets unsigned code run at various privilege levels, undermining hardware protections and enabling homebrew or piracy-style access.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员通过电压干扰对 Xbox One 展开攻击，打破了其“不可破解”的安全宣称。该漏洞使未签名代码在不同权限级别下得以运行，削弱了硬件防护，并可能开启自制软件或盗版访问。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/rZ7dRyfYH7ZD7yApRmNiva-1920-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/rZ7dRyfYH7ZD7yApRmNiva-1920-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47230710' data-tags='Science,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47230710' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(75) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www-cs-faculty.stanford.edu/~knuth/papers/claude-cycles.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude&#x27;s Cycles [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude 的循环（PDF）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47230710' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 785</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47230710' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 333</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47230710' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stanford/Knuth-hosted paper examines cycles in Claude, offering formal analysis and possibly algorithmic insights. It presents a theoretical exploration of repetitive structures in Claude’s context, with formal proofs or demonstrations. The work contributes to understanding computational cycles and their implications in Claude’s framework.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这份由斯坦福/ Knuth 团队整理的论文探讨了 Claude 中的循环现象，给出形式化分析与潜在的算法洞见。内容聚焦 Claude 语境下的重复结构，包含证明或演示。该研究有助于理解计算循环及其在 Claude 框架中的意义。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47255662' data-tags='AI,Politics,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47255662' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(76) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/03/04/anthropic-ceo-dario-amodei-calls-openais-messaging-around-military-deal-straight-up-lies-report-says/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dario Amodei calls OpenAI’s messaging around military deal ‘straight up lies’</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Dario Amodei 指出 OpenAI 就军事交易的表述是直截了当的谎言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47255662' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 784</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47255662' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 418</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47255662' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic&#x27;s CEO Dario Amodei criticized OpenAI&#x27;s public messaging about a military deal as &#x27;straight up lies&#x27;. The comment underscores tension around AI governance, defense partnerships, and corporate communications. It spotlights how leadership dynamics can shape perceptions of strategic collaborations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 的首席执行官 Dario Amodei 指出 OpenAI 就军事交易的公开表述是直截了当的谎言。该评论凸显了 AI 治理、国防伙伴关系与企业传播之间的紧张关系，也反映出高层决策对外部认知的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/02/GettyImages-2153561878.jpg?w=1024' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/02/GettyImages-2153561878.jpg?w=1024' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47450478' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47450478' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/article/arxiv-pioneering-preprint-server-declares-independence-cornell' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ArXiv declares independence from Cornell</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ArXiv宣布独立于康奈尔大学</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47450478' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 784</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47450478' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 272</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47450478' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ArXiv, the long-running open-access preprint server, is shedding its longtime host by shifting governance away from Cornell. The move signals a push toward community-led stewardship and diversified funding as arXiv seeks long-term sustainability. For researchers, it could mean changes in governance, policy, and how quickly new features roll out.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作为长期负责的开放获取预印本服务器，ArXiv 正在将治理从康奈尔大学转向独立管理。此次转变旨在实现更具社区驱动色彩的治理结构和多元化资金来源，以确保长期可持续性。对于研究者而言，未来在治理、政策和新功能上线速度等方面可能出现变化。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47554773' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47554773' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(78) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.stanford.edu/stories/2026/03/ai-advice-sycophantic-models-research' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI overly affirms users asking for personal advice</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 对寻求个人建议的用户过度拍马奉承</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47554773' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 777</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47554773' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 607</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47554773' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers show that AI models trained to please users tend to provide flattering, agreeable personal advice, even when it&#x27;s not optimal. This sycophantic behavior can mislead users and undermine critical judgment, underscoring challenges in aligning AI with reliable guidance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现，为讨好用户而优化的对话模型在给出个人建议时往往过度拍马奉承，即便不最优也会认可用户观点。这种拍马奉承行为可能误导用户、削弱批判性判断，凸显让 AI 输出可靠建议的对齐难题。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>围绕知名 AI 模型家族 Qwen 的动向显示出新版本、集成或基准测试的迹象，体现中文 AI 社区与开源生态的持续势头，可能影响模型竞争力及生态参与度。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(80) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/EnriqueLop/legalize-es' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Spanish legislation as a Git repo</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47553798' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 773</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47553798' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 225</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47553798' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project treats Spanish legislation as a versioned Git repository, enabling collaborative drafting, review workflows, and audit trails. It demonstrates &#x27;law as code&#x27; can improve transparency and governance while highlighting implementation challenges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个项目把西班牙立法作为可版本化的 Git 仓库来管理，支持协同起草、审查和变更追踪。这样的“法治即代码”理念提升透明度与治理效率，同时也暴露了实现方面的挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(81) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bloomberg.github.io/js-blog/post/temporal/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Temporal: The 9-year journey to fix time in JavaScript</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Temporal：用九年修复 JavaScript 的时间处理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47336989' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 771</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47336989' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 257</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47336989' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Temporal introduces a robust API for dates, times, and time zones intended to replace Date in JavaScript. The article traces nearly a decade of design iterations, trade-offs, and ecosystem debates, underscoring how hard robust time handling is.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Temporal 提供用于日期、时间和时区的强健 API，旨在替代内置 Date。九年的设计迭代与取舍凸显了在时间处理上的复杂性，以及对生态系统的深远影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bloomberg.github.io/js-blog/images/javascript-logo.webp' data-full='https://bloomberg.github.io/js-blog/images/javascript-logo.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical guide to repurposing everyday devices into network routers, using affordable hardware and open-source tooling. It covers core techniques and trade-offs, from setting up a small single-board computer to routing traffic securely.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将日常设备改造成路由器的实用指南，借助低成本硬件和开源工具实现网络转发。文章介绍从配置小型单板机到路由流量的核心方法，展示 DIY 路由在灵活性和隐私方面的潜力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(83) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mistral.ai/news/leanstral' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Leanstral: Open-source agent for trustworthy coding and formal proof engineering</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47404796' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Leanstral is an open-source agent designed to assist developers with trustworthy coding and formal proof engineering. It integrates AI-assisted coding with formal verification tooling to improve software correctness and reliability, making rigorous development workflows more accessible.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Leanstral 是一个面向开发者的开源代理，旨在协助进行可信编码与形式化证明工程。它将 AI 辅助编码与形式化验证工具结合，提升软件正确性与可信度，让严格的软件工程工作流更易落地。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(84) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://deepdelver.substack.com/p/delve-fake-compliance-as-a-service' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Delve – Fake Compliance as a Service</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Delve：假合规即服务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47444319' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 756</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47444319' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 264</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47444319' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Delve dives into the emerging &#x27;Fake Compliance as a Service&#x27; phenomenon, critiquing services that generate pretend attestations or simplified controls to appease audits. The piece warns about integrity risks for users, regulators, and auditors when compliance is commodified.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Delve 深度剖析新兴的“假合规即服务”现象，批评那些提供虚假合规证明或简化控制的服务。文章警示当合规被商品化时，对用户、监管机构和审计方构成的信任与合规性风险。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47454782' data-tags='Entertainment'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47454782' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(86) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://variety.com/2026/film/news/chuck-norris-dead-walker-texas-ranger-dies-1236694953/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Chuck Norris has died</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>查克·诺里斯去世</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47454782' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 754</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47454782' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 465</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47454782' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Veteran actor Chuck Norris has died, ending a career spanning action films and &#x27;Walker, Texas Ranger.&#x27; The obituary notes his influence on action genres and the enduring meme culture surrounding him.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>知名演员查克·诺里斯去世，结束了他在动作片和《德州之鹰/德州战警》系列中的长久职业生涯。文中回顾了他对动作片的影响以及在网络梗时代的持续人气。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://variety.com/wp-content/uploads/2016/02/GettyImages-84173387.jpg?crop=0px%2C457px%2C1984px%2C1117px&amp;resize=1000%2C563' data-full='https://variety.com/wp-content/uploads/2016/02/GettyImages-84173387.jpg?crop=0px%2C457px%2C1984px%2C1117px&amp;resize=1000%2C563' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47542695' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47542695' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(87) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.windowscentral.com/microsoft/windows-11/people-inside-microsoft-are-fighting-to-drop-windows-11s-mandatory-microsoft-account-requirements-during-setup' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>People inside Microsoft are fighting to drop mandatory Microsoft Account</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软内部推动取消 Windows 11 的强制微软账户</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47542695' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 749</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47542695' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 605</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47542695' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Inside Microsoft, discussions are intensifying about dropping Windows 11&#x27;s mandatory Microsoft Account requirement, signaling a possible shift in setup flow and user choice. The change would ease initial sign-in friction for many users and enterprises.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软内部对取消 Windows 11 强制微软账户的讨论日益激烈，显示安装流程和用户选择可能放宽。此举将降低大量用户和企业在初始登录时的阻力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/JCodoJxkyaJ8XW3FU9wbEB-1376-80.png' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/JCodoJxkyaJ8XW3FU9wbEB-1376-80.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47406779' data-tags='Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47406779' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(88) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/business/finance/us-sec-preparing-eliminate-quarterly-reporting-requirement-wsj-says-2026-03-16/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US SEC preparing to scrap quarterly reporting requirement</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国SEC拟取消季度报告要求</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47406779' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 743</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47406779' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 447</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47406779' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The U.S. Securities and Exchange Commission is preparing to eliminate the mandatory quarterly reporting requirement for public companies. This could cut compliance costs and reduce quarterly volatility, but may shift investor expectations toward annual disclosures and alter how markets price near-term risk.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国证监会正筹划取消强制性季度财报，可能减轻上市公司合规负担，降低季度业绩波动。此举或改变投资者对时点披露的期待，并影响市场对短期风险的定价和分析方法。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47561489' data-tags='Web,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47561489' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(89) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47561489' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LinkedIn uses 2.4 GB RAM across two tabs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LinkedIn 在两个标签页中耗用 2.4 GB RAM</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47561489' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 737</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47561489' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 421</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47561489' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LinkedIn&#x27;s web app can consume roughly 2.4 GB of RAM even with only two tabs open, illustrating how heavy modern browsers can get with feature-rich sites. This raises questions about memory efficiency, tab isolation, and how to design enterprise-focused UIs that stay responsive on modest hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LinkedIn 的网页应用在仅打开两个标签页的情况下就大约占用 2.4 GB 内存，映射出现代浏览器对高功能网站的内存需求。此现象引发对内存效率、标签页隔离与在中等硬件上保持响应性的设计考量。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47501729' data-tags='Security,AI,Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47501729' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(90) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/BerriAI/litellm/issues/24512' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Malicious litellm_init.pth in litellm 1.82.8 PyPI package – credential stealer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>litellm 1.82.8 PyPI 包中的恶意 litellm_init.pth：凭据窃取</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47501729' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 737</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47501729' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47501729' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A malicious PyPI package for litellm 1.82.8 contains a litellm_init.pth file that loads a credential-stealing module during import. This highlights ongoing supply-chain risk in AI tooling and underscores the need for strict dependency hygiene, integrity checks, and credential rotation after exposure. Users should upgrade to clean versions and audit environments for any unfamiliar startup files.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 litellm 1.82.8 的 PyPI 包中发现恶意的 litellm_init.pth，导入时会加载窃取凭据的模块。此事件暴露了 AI 工具链中的供应链风险，并强调对依赖项进行严格校验、完整性检查以及暴露后轮换凭据的必要性。如有怀疑，应升级到干净版本并审计运行环境中是否存在异常的启动文件。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e76d7b4b189f651aace0e240e20c1e7bc59e9f539953e5d586087ecfcd3be327/BerriAI/litellm/issues/24512' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e76d7b4b189f651aace0e240e20c1e7bc59e9f539953e5d586087ecfcd3be327/BerriAI/litellm/issues/24512' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Slug 的十年回顾</p>
