<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47508745' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47508745' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(92) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.jakesaunders.dev/is-anybody-else-bored-of-talking-about-ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Is anybody else bored of talking about AI?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>还有人觉得聊 AI 吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47508745' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 728</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47508745' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 514</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47508745' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A reflective essay questions whether the constant AI chatter has worn people down and argues for focusing on practical, real-world impact over hype. It pushes readers to value concrete use cases, governance, and measurable progress rather than trending headlines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇随笔质疑持续的 AI 热议是否让人疲惫，并主张把重点放在现实世界的实际影响而非炒作。作者鼓励关注具体的使用场景、治理与可衡量的进展，而不是追逐热度新闻。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.jakesaunders.dev/is-anybody-else-bored-of-talking-about-ai/img.png' data-full='https://blog.jakesaunders.dev/is-anybody-else-bored-of-talking-about-ai/img.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47356968' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47356968' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(93) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.grandforksherald.com/news/north-dakota/ai-error-jails-innocent-grandmother-for-months-in-north-dakota-fraud-case' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Innocent woman jailed after being misidentified using AI facial recognition</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>因AI人脸识别误识而入狱的无辜老妇人</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47356968' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 726</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47356968' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 376</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47356968' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An innocent grandmother spent months in jail after facial recognition wrongly identified her as a fraud suspect. The case underscores the high-stakes risk of relying on imperfect AI for law enforcement, where false positives can ruin lives and erode trust. It adds to ongoing debates about accuracy, bias, and accountability in AI-powered policing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位无辜的老妇人在人脸识别错误识别后被关押数月。此案凸显在执法中依赖不完美的人工智能所带来的高风险——误判可能摧毁个人生活并侵蚀公众信任。事件推动有关准确性、偏见与对AI警务的问责制度的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.forumcomm.com/dims4/default/ebb8316/2147483647/strip/true/crop/1280x720+0+0/resize/1895x1066!/quality/90/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fforum-communications-production-web.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com%2Fbrightspot%2Fce%2F45%2F16d552fb49c78f0fa3b370e2dd0f%2Fmattwebpics-still001.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.forumcomm.com/dims4/default/ebb8316/2147483647/strip/true/crop/1280x720+0+0/resize/1895x1066!/quality/90/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fforum-communications-production-web.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com%2Fbrightspot%2Fce%2F45%2F16d552fb49c78f0fa3b370e2dd0f%2Fmattwebpics-still001.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47504112' data-tags='Business,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47504112' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(94) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/03/introducing-apple-business-a-new-all-in-one-platform-for-businesses-of-all-sizes/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple Business</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Apple Business：面向各规模企业的一体化平台</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47504112' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 724</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47504112' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 423</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47504112' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple introduces Apple Business, an all-in-one platform to streamline device management, security, and app deployment for organizations of all sizes. By consolidating IT controls and enterprise workflows, it aims to reduce friction for Apple-powered ecosystems in the workplace. The move could reshape enterprise IT purchasing and cross-device collaboration strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果推出 Apple Business，一站式平台用于简化企业级设备管理、安全性与应用部署，覆盖各类规模的组织。通过整合 IT 控制和企业工作流，意在降低在工作场景中使用苹果设备的门槛。这一举措可能影响企业 IT 的采购决策及跨设备协作方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/03/introducing-apple-business-a-new-all-in-one-platform-for-businesses-of-all-sizes/tile/Apple-Business-hero-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202603241459' data-full='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/03/introducing-apple-business-a-new-all-in-one-platform-for-businesses-of-all-sizes/tile/Apple-Business-hero-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202603241459' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47253177' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47253177' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(95) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bill.newgrounds.com/news/post/1607118' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Building a new Flash</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 Newgrounds 上打造全新 Flash 替代品</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47253177' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 722</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47253177' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 234</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47253177' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project aims to build a new Flash-like experience on modern platforms, reviving the classic animation and game ecosystem from the old web. It highlights the community&#x27;s desire for open, accessible tooling and the challenges of sustaining a Flash-era ecosystem in today’s tech landscape.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个项目旨在在现代平台上打造类似 Flash 的全新体验，以复兴旧网页时代的动画和游戏生态。它凸显社区对开放、易获取工具的渴望，以及在当今技术环境中维持 Flash 时代生态所面临的挑战。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47548243' data-tags='Privacy,AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47548243' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(96) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47548243' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>If you don&#x27;t opt out by Apr 24 GitHub will train on your private repos</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如果你在4月24日前不选择退出，GitHub 将在你的私有仓库上用于训练</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47548243' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 721</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47548243' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 310</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47548243' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Developers face a policy change: if you don&#x27;t opt out by the deadline, GitHub will use contents of private repos to train its AI models. This raises privacy and IP concerns for individual developers and teams, and highlights the trade-off between model quality and data rights; opting out may require action and may affect tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>如果你未在截止日期前完成退出，GitHub 将把私有仓库的代码用于训练其人工智能模型。这将引发对隐私和知识产权的担忧，并可能影响企业对代码资产的控制。开发者需要了解退出流程与潜在影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47307124' data-tags='Culture,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47307124' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(97) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sharif.io/looking-stupid' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Willingness to look stupid</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>敢于出丑的勇气</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47307124' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 720</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47307124' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 247</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47307124' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Embracing the possibility of looking stupid accelerates learning and innovation. It encourages rapid experimentation, honest feedback, and psychological safety in teams. The piece argues that the payoff from taking small risks often outweighs the embarrassment of initial missteps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>敢于出丑的勇气可以加速学习和创新。它促使团队进行快速试错、直率反馈，并提升心理安全感。作者认为从小型风险中获得的收益往往超过初次失误带来的尴尬。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://api.sharif.io/content/images/2026/03/IMG_1749-1.jpg' data-full='https://api.sharif.io/content/images/2026/03/IMG_1749-1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47397593' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47397593' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(98) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.frontiersin.org/journals/political-science/articles/10.3389/fpos.2026.1779810/full' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Corruption erodes social trust more in democracies than in autocracies</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>腐败在民主国家对社会信任的侵蚀比专制国家更严重</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47397593' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 717</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47397593' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 380</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47397593' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new study finds corruption erodes social trust more in democratic countries than in autocracies. The result suggests that transparency and accountability mechanisms in democracies can backfire when corruption spreads, hurting trust in institutions more than in tightly controlled regimes. The finding matters for governance, civic tech, and how platforms rely on public trust.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新研究显示，腐败对社会信任的侵蚀在民主国家比在专制国家更明显。这一发现提示，民主制度的透明与问责机制在某些情境下可能放大信任的下滑，对治理、公民科技以及依赖信任的平台都具重要影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://d2csxpduxe849s.cloudfront.net/media/E32629C6-9347-4F84-81FEAEF7BFA342B3/CF13FC93-1D3A-473F-8D8F9F692E417F16/5974D84F-C240-47A0-B44DDB68A3E39BD6/WebsiteWebP_XL-FPOS_Main Visual_Purple_Website.webp' data-full='https://d2csxpduxe849s.cloudfront.net/media/E32629C6-9347-4F84-81FEAEF7BFA342B3/CF13FC93-1D3A-473F-8D8F9F692E417F16/5974D84F-C240-47A0-B44DDB68A3E39BD6/WebsiteWebP_XL-FPOS_Main Visual_Purple_Website.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

It signals growing competition in AI infrastructure tooling beyond pure model size.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mistral AI 发布 Forge，旨在简化 AI 模型开发与部署的工具包。该平台强调高性能与开放性，帮助开发者更高效地组装、训练和部署模型，体现了在模型之外的 AI 基础设施竞争加剧。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(100) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/anemll/status/2035901335984611412' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>iPhone 17 Pro Demonstrated Running a 400B LLM</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47490070' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 700</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47490070' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 322</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An iPhone 17 Pro demonstration shows a 400-billion-parameter LLM running locally on-device, signaling advances in edge AI and privacy-preserving inference. The feat highlights model compression, efficient on-device runtimes, and the potential shift away from cloud-only AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一段演示显示 iPhone 17 Pro 能在本地设备上运行 400B 参数的 LLM，标志边缘 AI 的进展与隐私保护推理能力。此成就凸显模型压缩、边缘运行效率的提升，以及 AI 由云端依赖向本地推理的潜在转变。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>氦气供应中断威胁半导体生产，可能延迟晶圆厂与提高成本。行业正在推动多源采购与替代气体策略来提升韧性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47564421' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(105) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techfixated.com/a-1977-time-capsule-voyager-1-runs-on-69-kb-of-memory-and-an-8-track-tape-recorder-4/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Voyager 1 runs on 69 KB of memory and an 8-track tape recorder</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>旅行者1号仅靠69KB内存与8轨磁带记录器运行</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47564421' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 673</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47564421' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 253</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47564421' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Voyager 1 still operates with a mere 69 KB of RAM and an 8-track tape recorder, a testament to long-lived space hardware engineered decades ago. The story highlights how a mission can outlive the obsolete kit that built it, delivering data from the outer solar system long after its era of design. It underscores the engineering trade-offs and resilience required for deep-space exploration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>旅行者1号仍以仅有的69 KB RAM和8轨磁带记录器运行，展示了数十年前设计的硬件的持久耐用。这个故事凸显了任务往往超越其设计时的技术极限，能够持续传回外太阳系的数据，凸显深空探测的工程权衡与韧性。此事也让人反思长期任务对老旧组件的依赖与替换挑战。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47529609' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47529609' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(107) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.patrick-breyer.de/en/end-of-chat-control-eu-parliament-stops-mass-surveillance-in-voting-thriller-paving-the-way-for-genuine-child-protection/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>End of &quot;Chat Control&quot;: EU parliament stops mass surveillance</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《聊天控制》终结：欧盟议会阻止大规模监控，为真正的儿童保护铺路</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47529609' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 669</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47529609' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 308</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47529609' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EU Parliament&#x27;s stance on Chat Control signals an end to mass surveillance in that policy area, paving the way for targeted child-protection measures. The vote reframes privacy policy and could influence how Europe approaches open web safety.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟议会对 Chat Control 的态度表明停止大规模监控的方向，为以目标化手段保护儿童铺路。此举重塑隐私政策，并可能影响欧洲在开放网络安全方面的做法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.patrick-breyer.de/wp-content/uploads/2023/07/logo_regular_vertical.png' data-full='https://www.patrick-breyer.de/wp-content/uploads/2023/07/logo_regular_vertical.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47386703' data-tags='Open Source,Science,Space,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47386703' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(108) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openrocket.info/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenRocket</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenRocket：开源火箭设计与仿真工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47386703' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 656</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47386703' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 113</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47386703' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenRocket is an open-source rocket design and flight simulation package that lets hobbyists, students, and researchers model aerodynamics, stability, and trajectories before launch. It helps verify designs and teaches rocketry principles without expensive hardware. The project embodies the practical value of open scientific tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenRocket 是一个开源的火箭设计与飞行仿真工具，用户能在发射前建模气动、稳定性和轨迹。它便于爱好者、学生和研究者在没有昂贵设备的情况下验证设计、学习火箭学原理。该项目体现了开源科学工具的实用价值。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://openrocket.info/img/OpenRocket_icon.png' data-full='https://openrocket.info/img/OpenRocket_icon.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47495527' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A printable Claude Code cheat sheet that auto-updates daily, serving as a handy quick-reference for prompts, patterns, and tips when coding with Claude. It&#x27;s a lightweight, always-current resource for frequent Claude Code users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一份可打印的 Claude Code 快速参考表每日自动更新，提供提示语、常用模式和编码技巧，便于在使用 Claude 编码时快速查阅。该资源保持最新，适合高频使用者。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47322635' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47322635' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(110) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnbc.com/2026/03/08/social-media-child-safety-internet-ai-surveillance.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Online age-verification tools for child safety are surveilling adults</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>面向儿童安全的线上年龄验证工具正在监控成年人</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47322635' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 652</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47322635' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 339</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47322635' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Age-verification tools designed for child safety are collecting data on adults, creating surveillance for a broader audience. The piece argues for privacy-centric designs and stricter data governance to prevent abuse.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>为儿童安全而设计的在线年龄验证工具，正在对成年用户进行数据收集和监控，带来隐私风险。需要在设计和治理层面加强隐私保护，避免对成年人造成不必要的监控。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.cnbcfm.com/api/v1/image/108270637-1772139080188-gettyimages-2261615361-260216_TFEA_PHT022.jpeg?v=1772139104&amp;w=1920&amp;h=1080' data-full='https://image.cnbcfm.com/api/v1/image/108270637-1772139080188-gettyimages-2261615361-260216_TFEA_PHT022.jpeg?v=1772139104&amp;w=1920&amp;h=1080' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47478401' data-tags='Programming,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47478401' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(111) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bramcohen.com/p/manyana' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The future of version control</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>版本控制的未来</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47478401' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 652</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47478401' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 371</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47478401' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Visionaries describe the next phase of version control beyond Git-style systems, spotlighting peer-to-peer collaboration, cryptographic integrity, and scalable, offline-friendly workflows. The takeaway is that new paradigms could change how teams coordinate, verify history, and scale development. For technologists, this signals a shift in toolchains and trust conventions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讨论了超越现有版本控制系统的新范式，强调点对点协作、加密完整性和可离线扩展的工作流。核心在于新机制可能改变团队的协作、历史验证和开发扩展的方式。对开发者来说，这预示着工具链与信任体系的变革。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!93xj!,f_auto,q_auto:best,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbramcohen.substack.com%2Ftwitter%2Fsubscribe-card.jpg%3Fv%3D-1733548744%26version%3D9' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!93xj!,f_auto,q_auto:best,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbramcohen.substack.com%2Ftwitter%2Fsubscribe-card.jpg%3Fv%3D-1733548744%26version%3D9' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Plasma Bigscreen：KDE Plasma 的十英尺界面</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>KDE 团队在尝试将桌面体验扩展到十英尺的客厅界面，聚焦电视友好型导航、遥控输入以及以媒体为中心的工作流，重新定义大屏幕上的桌面使用方式。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(114) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://acko.net/blog/the-l-in-llm-stands-for-lying/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The L in &quot;LLM&quot; Stands for Lying</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47257394' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that large language models frequently produce incorrect or deceptive outputs, challenging developers to evaluate reliability and trust.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>认为大型语言模型经常产生不正确或误导性的输出，敦促开发者评估可靠性与信任度。文章探讨模型能力与真实度之间的权衡，以及从业者应当衡量的指标。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47535708' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47535708' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(116) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://9to5mac.com/2026/03/26/apple-discontinues-the-mac-pro/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple discontinues the Mac Pro</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果停产 Mac Pro</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47535708' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 642</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47535708' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 616</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47535708' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple has ended the Mac Pro line, signaling a shift away from modular Xeon-based workstations toward higher-density consumer-grade hardware or external compute options. Professionals may pivot to Mac Studio or other external compute solutions, reshaping workflows that depend on PCIe expandability and upgradeability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果宣布停产 Mac Pro，这标志着其工作站路线向高密度消费级硬件或外部计算方案的转变。专业用户可能转向 Mac Studio 或其他外设扩展解决方案，升级与扩展性成为新关注点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/9to5mac.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/6/2026/03/mac-pro-rip.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/9to5mac.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/6/2026/03/mac-pro-rip.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47323017' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47323017' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(117) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/ai/2026/03/after-outages-amazon-to-make-senior-engineers-sign-off-on-ai-assisted-changes/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>After outages, Amazon to make senior engineers sign off on AI-assisted changes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>故障后，亚马逊要求高级工程师对AI辅助变更签字批准</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47323017' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 642</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47323017' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 475</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47323017' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Amazon introduces governance requiring senior engineers to approve AI-assisted changes before deployment after outages. This aims to improve safety, accountability, and rollback capabilities for critical systems in an AI-augmented ops environment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>为加强关键系统的安全性和可追溯性，亚马逊宣布在故障后实施 AI 辅助变更需高级工程师签字批准的治理规则，确保可回滚与问责。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/GettyImages-1157406884-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2019/09/GettyImages-1157406884-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47389570' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47389570' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(118) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stopsloppypasta.ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stop Sloppypasta</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Stop Sloppypasta</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47389570' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 642</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47389570' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 247</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47389570' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stop Sloppypasta is a project advocating higher-quality AI-generated content and practices. It offers guidelines or tools for better prompting, attribution, and quality checks. Takeaway: as AI-generated content proliferates, tools and norms for quality matter more than ever.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Stop Sloppypasta 致力于提升 AI 生成内容的质量与负责任的使用，提供更好的提示设计、署名与质量检查的指南与工具。要点：随着 AI 内容的泛滥，质量标准与工具变得比以往更为重要。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47555556' data-tags='Security,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>勒蒙德通过健身应用实现法国航母的实时定位</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47453942' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 629</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47453942' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 514</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47453942' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Le Monde investigation shows real-time tracking of France&#x27;s aircraft carrier via fitness app data, revealing how public location sharing can leak sensitive security information.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>勒蒙德的调查显示，公开的健身应用数据可能让法国航母的位置在实时暴露，暴露了公共数据对国家安全的潜在威胁。此事凸显了个人位置信息在缺乏隐私保护的情况下对安全的影响，促使对数据共享进行审视。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提供从 GitHub 迁移到 Codeberg 的低成本路径，面向不愿在迁移上花太多精力的开发者。着重实用步骤与取舍，展示 Codeberg 作为开源友好托管平台的潜在优势，尤其适合懒人和小团队。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A short essay argues for extending hardware lifespans through repairable design, modular upgrades, and stronger anti-obsolescence norms. It warns that consumer electronics waste grows as devices become harder to repair, urging both makers and buyers to rethink responsibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文主张通过可维修设计、模块化升级和更强的反废弃规范来延长硬件寿命，警示随电子产品推陈出新导致的电子废弃物增加。作者呼吁厂商与用户共同重塑责任感，以减缓浪费并提升可持续性。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47519519' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Officials in Slovenia accuse the private intelligence firm Black Cube of attempting to influence a European election through covert tactics and deceptive investors. The case highlights evolving concerns about influence operations in democracies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>斯洛文尼亚官员指控私营情报公司 Black Cube 通过隐蔽手段和虚假投资者试图影响欧洲选举。这一案件凸显了民主国家中影响行动的持续担忧。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47335572' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 619</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47335572' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 276</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47335572' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A whistleblower alleges a former DOGE member copied Social Security data and brought it to a new job. The claim highlights insider risk and weak data-handling practices, underscoring the need for stronger access controls, monitoring, and data exfiltration safeguards in organizations handling sensitive numbers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名举报者声称，前 DOGE 成员将社会安全号码数据带到新工作岗位。此事暴露了内部威胁与数据处理的薄弱环节，强调在处理敏感信息的组织中需加强访问控制、审计与数据外泄防护。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47269263' data-tags='AI,Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47269263' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(131) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/news/where-stand-department-war' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Where things stand with the Department of War</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>战争部进展情况</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47269263' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 618</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47269263' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 769</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47269263' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic provides an update on its collaboration framed as the &#x27;Department of War&#x27; program, outlining milestones, security measures, and engagements with government partners. The update highlights how safety and governance considerations shape public-sector AI deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 提供了关于名为“战争部”的项目的最新进展，概述了关键里程碑、安全措施以及与政府伙伴的合作。更新强调了安全治理在公共部门 AI 部署中的作用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/faeb5935baa3fba4e4d4459637e83e14d218b648-2400x1260.png' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/faeb5935baa3fba4e4d4459637e83e14d218b648-2400x1260.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47263595' data-tags='Security,Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47263595' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(132) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://grith.ai/blog/clinejection-when-your-ai-tool-installs-another' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A GitHub Issue Title Compromised 4k Developer Machines</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一个 GitHub 问题标题被劫持，影响约4千台开发者机器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47263595' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 615</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47263595' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 190</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47263595' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A blog suggests that a crafted GitHub issue title could trigger an injection in an AI-assisted workflow, potentially compromising thousands of developer machines. The post underscores the need for input sanitization, strict tool boundaries, and provenance verification when AI is integrated with code hosting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇博文指出，通过设计的 GitHub issue 标题可能在 AI 助手工作流中触发命令注入，进而影响数千名开发者的机器。此事强调在将 AI 与代码托管服务结合使用时需加强输入过滤、边界控制和来源可信度的验证。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://grith.ai/blog/clinejection-when-your-ai-tool-installs-another/hero-clinejection-chain-1600x900.png' data-full='https://grith.ai/blog/clinejection-when-your-ai-tool-installs-another/hero-clinejection-chain-1600x900.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47331811' data-tags='Programming,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47331811' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(133) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hacks.mozilla.org/2026/02/making-webassembly-a-first-class-language-on-the-web/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Making WebAssembly a first-class language on the Web</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>让 WebAssembly 成为 Web 上的一等语言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47331811' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 615</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47331811' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 227</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47331811' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Efforts to make WebAssembly a first-class language on the Web focus on richer runtime features—like GC, component model, interface types, and better debugging—to let high-level languages interoperate and be authored for the browser without forcing JavaScript as the only host. If successful, this work would broaden language choice for web apps, improve performance portability, and unlock tooling and deployment patterns closer to native languages.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将 WebAssembly 打造成 Web 上的一等语言需要补齐运行时功能（如垃圾回收、组件模型、接口类型和更好的调试支持），以便高阶语言能无缝互操作并原生面向浏览器。若能实现，这将扩展网页的语言选择、提升性能可移植性，并带来更接近本地开发的工具链和部署方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://hacks.mozilla.org/wp-content/themes/Hax/img/hacks-meta-image.jpg' data-full='https://hacks.mozilla.org/wp-content/themes/Hax/img/hacks-meta-image.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47252049' data-tags='Privacy,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47252049' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(134) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://deflock.org/map#map=5/37.125286/-96.284180' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An interactive map of Flock Cams</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Flock 摄像头互动地图</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47252049' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 613</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47252049' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 233</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47252049' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A map visualizes the locations and coverage of Flock’s camera network, providing transparency into surveillance tech used in public or semi-public spaces. It raises privacy considerations, as more data about camera placement becomes publicly accessible. The project demonstrates how mapping can contextualize safety tech in communities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一张地图展示了 Flock 摄像头网络的分布与覆盖范围，使公众能够更直观看到用于公共或半公共空间的监控技术。随之而来的，是对隐私的关切，因为摄像头布点等信息更加透明可见。该项目展示了如何通过地图来理解和评估社区中的安防技术。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://deflock.org/og-banner.png' data-full='https://deflock.org/og-banner.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47434047' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47434047' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(135) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://haskellforall.com/2026/03/a-sufficiently-detailed-spec-is-code' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A sufficiently detailed spec is code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>足够详细的规格就是代码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47434047' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 611</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47434047' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 327</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47434047' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Detailed specifications can serve as concrete, testable artifacts that drive implementation without separate code. When specs are precise, teams can simulate behavior and verify outcomes directly from the docs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>足够详尽的规格本身就能成为可执行的产物，推动实现、测试与仿真，模糊了文档与代码的界线。把规范当作代码来管理，可以用自动化校验与模拟来验证行为。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://haskellforall.com/2026/03/a-sufficiently-detailed-spec-is-code/opengraph-image?133960dfd41055ac' data-full='https://haskellforall.com/2026/03/a-sufficiently-detailed-spec-is-code/opengraph-image?133960dfd41055ac' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(137) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://grapheneos.social/@GrapheneOS/116261301913660830' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GrapheneOS will remain usable by anyone without requiring personal information</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47482217' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 604</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47482217' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 186</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47482217' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GrapheneOS reiterates its privacy-first approach, stressing that you can use the OS without providing personal information. The note highlights the trade-offs in keeping the ecosystem usable without sign-ins or telemetry, and what this means for typical users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GrapheneOS 继续坚持隐私优先理念，强调无需提供个人信息即可使用系统。文章点出在保持无需账户或数据传输的前提下，生态系统所需的权衡以及对普通用户的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(140) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://action.wemove.eu/sign/2026-03-palantir-petition-EN' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Say No to Palantir in Europe</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Say No to Palantir 在欧洲</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47563655' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 601</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47563655' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 168</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47563655' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A European grassroots petition urges policymakers to curb Palantir&#x27;s analytics footprint amid privacy and surveillance concerns. The campaign highlights growing friction between AI/data brokers and European data protection norms, potentially shaping procurement and vendor choices across the public and private sectors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在欧洲兴起的民间请愿呼吁监管机构控制 Palantir 的数据分析影响，强调隐私与监控方面的担忧。该运动凸显数据经纪商与欧洲数据保护规范之间的博弈，或影响公共与私营部门的采购与供应商选择。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://wemove.cdn.actionkit.com/images/Palantir_petition_image.png' data-full='https://wemove.cdn.actionkit.com/images/Palantir_petition_image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47550282' data-tags='AI,Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47550282' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(141) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jai.scs.stanford.edu/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Go hard on agents, not on your filesystem</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>把精力放在代理上，而不是文件系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47550282' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 600</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47550282' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 312</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47550282' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This view argues that you should design around lightweight agents that orchestrate tasks and data flows rather than stuffing complexity into the filesystem. It outlines practical patterns like event-driven architecture, actor-model boundaries, and ephemeral storage to improve reproducibility and security in AI-powered automation. Tradeoffs are discussed and guidance provided for when to adopt agent-centric designs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该观点主张围绕轻量代理来编排任务和数据流，而不是把复杂性堆在文件系统上。文中给出事件驱动架构、演员模型边界、瞬态存储等实用模式，以提升可重复性、安全性和在 AI 自动化中的可组合性。还讨论了权衡以及在何种场景应采用以代理为中心的设计。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://jai.scs.stanford.edu/assets/logo.C7a5JZI0.svg' data-full='https://jai.scs.stanford.edu/assets/logo.C7a5JZI0.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47273854' data-tags='Security,AI,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47273854' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(142) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/news/mozilla-firefox-security' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hardening Firefox with Anthropic&#x27;s Red Team</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>与 Anthropic 红队联合加强 Firefox 安全</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47273854' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 599</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47273854' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 165</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47273854' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mozilla recruited Anthropic&#x27;s red team to test Firefox against adversarial threats, uncovering vulnerabilities and proposing hardening strategies. The effort demonstrates how external red-team exercises can raise browser security in a era of AI-assisted attacks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mozilla 邀请 Anthropic 红队对 Firefox 进行对抗性测试，揭示漏洞并提出加固策略。此举展示了在 AI 驱动威胁日益猖獗的背景下，外部红队演练如何提升浏览器安全性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.mozilla.org/wp-content/blogs.dir/278/files/2026/03/Mozilla_Illustrations_Pixelgram_Lock_Square_Orange-1080x720.png' data-full='https://blog.mozilla.org/wp-content/blogs.dir/278/files/2026/03/Mozilla_Illustrations_Pixelgram_Lock_Square_Orange-1080x720.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47274676' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47274676' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(143) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.cornell.edu/stories/2026/03/workers-who-love-synergizing-paradigms-might-be-bad-their-jobs' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Workers who love ‘synergizing paradigms’ might be bad at their jobs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>喜欢‘协同范式’的员工可能不适合本职工作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47274676' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 597</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47274676' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 327</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47274676' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Cornell piece suggests that teams heavy on buzzwords like synergizing paradigms may underperform, emphasizing concrete problem-solving over abstract rhetoric. The message resonates in tech culture where clear outcomes beat grand metaphors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cornell 的研究称，过分依赖“协同范式”等空洞术语的员工可能业绩不佳，强调解决问题的具体性比空谈更重要。这一观点在科技团队中尤为相关。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.cornell.edu/sites/default/files/styles/story_thumbnail_lg/public/2026-03/0302_corporatebs_tnd.jpg?itok=HH3QjqWe' data-full='https://news.cornell.edu/sites/default/files/styles/story_thumbnail_lg/public/2026-03/0302_corporatebs_tnd.jpg?itok=HH3QjqWe' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47291876' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47291876' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(144) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=qZuR-772cks' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microscopes can see video on a laserdisc</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>显微镜也能读出激光光盘上的视频</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47291876' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 593</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47291876' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 84</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47291876' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A quirky demonstration shows microscopic observation can reveal the video encoded on a LaserDisc by analyzing the physical grooves. The stunt illustrates how data can be recovered from old media and invites reflection on data preservation and analog-to-digital interfaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个有趣的演示显示，通过观察激光光盘的物理凹槽，显微镜可以还原其中的视频信息。这提醒人们关注旧媒体的数据保存与模拟到数字接口的转化。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47525243' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical set of shell tricks to automate chores, reduce repetition, and improve reliability, including safe globbing, smarter aliases, and convenient one-liners.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>实用的 shell 技巧合集，涵盖简短命令、别名管理与更安全的脚本实践，旨在降低重复劳动、提高开发者在终端中的生产力与心态稳定。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47582984' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(146) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/2026/03/30/github_copilot_ads_pull_requests/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GitHub backs down, kills Copilot pull-request ads after backlash</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub 在遭遇批评后取消 Copilot 拉取请求广告</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47582984' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 588</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47582984' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 362</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47582984' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GitHub has scrapped Copilot pull-request ads after community backlash, signaling a shift in how sponsor messaging appears inside developer workflows. The move adds to ongoing debates about paid AI assistance in open-source tooling and PR review noise.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GitHub 在社区强烈反对后取消 Copilot 在拉取请求中的广告，体现了开发者工作流中赞助信息呈现的权衡。此举加剧了关于在开源工具中使用付费 AI 助手以及降低 PR 审核噪声的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://regmedia.co.uk/2024/05/21/github1_shutterstock.jpg' data-full='https://regmedia.co.uk/2024/05/21/github1_shutterstock.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(147) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://larstofus.com/2026/03/22/the-gold-standard-of-optimization-a-look-under-the-hood-of-rollercoaster-tycoon/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The gold standard of optimization: A look under the hood of RollerCoaster Tycoon</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>优化的黄金标准：一探 RollerCoaster Tycoon 的底层实现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47480886' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 588</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47480886' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 159</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47480886' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A deep dive into the optimization practices behind RollerCoaster Tycoon, the piece argues that the game&#x27;s engine exemplifies rigorous, scalable performance engineering. It reveals the tradeoffs between simulation fidelity and render performance, offering lessons for real-time systems and game AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 RollerCoaster Tycoon 的底层优化实践进行深入解读，认为游戏引擎体现了严格且可扩展的性能工程。揭示仿真精度与渲染性能之间的权衡，为实时系统和游戏 AI 提供可借鉴的经验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://larstofus.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/rctarticlethumbnail.png' data-full='https://larstofus.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/rctarticlethumbnail.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(148) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.projectnomad.us' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Project Nomad – Knowledge That Never Goes Offline</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Project Nomad：知识永不离线</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47476821' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 588</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47476821' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 216</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47476821' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Presents an offline-first knowledge initiative designed to keep information accessible during outages or censorship. Emphasizes resilience and portability of knowledge through local-first data strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍一个离线优先的知识项目，确保在断网或审查环境中也能访问信息，强调通过本地化数据策略提升知识的韧性与可携带性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47226608' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47226608' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(149) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://futurism.com/artificial-intelligence/ars-technica-fires-reporter-ai-quotes' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ars Technica fires reporter after AI controversy involving fabricated quotes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ars Technica 因 AI 造假引发的争议解雇记者</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47226608' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 586</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47226608' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 367</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47226608' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A journalist is fired after allegations that quotes attributed to AI were fabricated. The incident underscores the need for rigorous verification when using AI in journalism and the ethics of AI-assisted reporting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>因涉及 AI 捏造引述的争议，记者被解雇。这一事件凸显在新闻报道中使用 AI 时，需进行严格的核实与伦理考量，以及对AI生成内容的信任风险。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47390817' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(150) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://developer.chrome.com/blog/chrome-devtools-mcp-debug-your-browser-session' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Chrome DevTools MCP (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Chrome DevTools 的 MCP（2025）——调试浏览器会话的新方式</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47390817' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 586</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47390817' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 231</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47390817' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Chrome DevTools introduces MCP-based tooling to debug browser sessions more comprehensively, enabling cross-process inspection and more seamless workflows for developers. The update aims to improve transparency and troubleshooting in complex web apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Chrome DevTools 推出 2025 版的 MCP 调试工作流，使开发者能够更全面地调试浏览器会话、跨进程诊断问题。新工 具提升了对复杂网页应用的可观测性与排错效率。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://developer.chrome.com/static/blog/chrome-devtools-mcp-debug-your-browser-session/image/hero.png' data-full='https://developer.chrome.com/static/blog/chrome-devtools-mcp-debug-your-browser-session/image/hero.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47476315' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47476315' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(151) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stevekrouse.com/precision' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Reports of code&#x27;s death are greatly exaggerated</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>关于代码死亡的传闻被大大夸大</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47476315' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 585</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47476315' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 433</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47476315' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Code isn&#x27;t dead; AI tools will augment but not replace human software craftsmen. The piece argues that precise, maintainable code, rigorous testing, and clear design remain essential as automation shifts routine work. In practice, expect a continued emphasis on tooling that preserves correctness and developer control.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尽管有 AI 辅助写码的趋势，代码并未消亡。高质量、可维护的代码、严谨的测试和明确的设计仍然是关键，因为自动化会改变工作方式但需要人类把控。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://imagedelivery.net/iHX6Ovru0O7AjmyT5yZRoA/f7e9e621-0fad-4c3a-a65b-d16832c02b00/public' data-full='https://imagedelivery.net/iHX6Ovru0O7AjmyT5yZRoA/f7e9e621-0fad-4c3a-a65b-d16832c02b00/public' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(152) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wired.com/story/yann-lecun-raises-dollar1-billion-to-build-ai-that-understands-the-physical-world/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Yann LeCun raises $1B to build AI that understands the physical world</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Yann LeCun 筹集十亿美元打造理解物理世界的 AI</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47320600' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 583</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47320600' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 470</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47320600' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Yann LeCun launches a billion-dollar fund to build AI systems that integrate perception, reasoning, and physical-world understanding. The effort signals a push toward grounding AI in real-world sensors and physics-based reasoning, with potential robotics implications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Yann LeCun 领衔的十亿美元基金，目标打造具备感知、推理和物理世界理解能力的 AI 系统。该方向强调把 AI 与现实世界传感与物理规律结合，可能带来机器人等应用的突破。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(153) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tinygrad.org/#tinybox' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tinybox – A powerful computer for deep learning</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tinybox：用于深度学习的强大小型电脑</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47470773' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 583</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47470773' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 339</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47470773' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tinybox packs substantial compute into a compact box, enabling on-device deep learning experiments without relying on the cloud. It targets researchers, hobbyists, and edge deployments seeking serious compute with a small footprint.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Tinybox 将强大算力压缩进小巧盒子，支持本地深度学习试验，无需依赖云端。面向希望在紧凑空间内获得高性能计算的研究者、爱好者与边缘部署。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47334293' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An opinion piece examines the concept and implications of a &#x27;MacBook Neo&#x27;—how new design choices, feature trade-offs, and product positioning might reshape Apple&#x27;s laptop line and user expectations. It considers where Apple might innovate versus where customers will expect continuity in performance, ports, and battery life.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇评论性文章探讨了“MacBook Neo”这一概念及其影响，讨论新的设计取舍和产品定位如何可能重塑苹果笔记本系列与用户预期。文章权衡了苹果可能带来的创新与用户对性能、接口和续航连续性的期望。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(155) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sightlessscribbles.com/posts/the-paperwork-flood/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The &#x27;paperwork flood&#x27;: How I drowned a bureaucrat before dinner</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47542057' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 578</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47542057' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 472</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47542057' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A humorous, first-person account of wrestling with bureaucratic red tape as the author builds a metaphorical &#x27;paperwork flood&#x27;. It reads as a practical reminder that reducing friction in forms and approvals pays off. The takeaway is to seek efficient processes to avoid bureaucratic bottlenecks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇以“纸上洪流”为题的博客故事，讲述作者如何对付繁琐的官僚流程。通过一连串讽刺与经验教训，揭示繁文缛节的痛点及应对思路。核心在于在文书工作中寻求高效、低摩擦的处理方式，避免被繁琐拖垮。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(157) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://help.obsidian.md/sync/headless' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Obsidian Sync now has a headless client</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Obsidian Sync 现已推出无头客户端</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47197267' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 574</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47197267' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 211</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47197267' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Obsidian Sync now offers a headless client, enabling automation and UI-free integrations. Teams can script automated syncs, run schedules, and build custom tools around their notes. This broadens Obsidian into programmable knowledge management beyond personal note-taking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Obsidian Sync 現已推出無頭客戶端，方便自動化與無界面整合。團隊可透過腳本自動同步、排程，並打造與筆記相關的自訂工具，讓知識管理更具可程式化性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ogimage.obsidian.md/og-image.png?title=Headless+Sync&amp;description=Obsidian+Sync+offers+a+headless+client+to+sync+vaults+without+using+the+desktop+app.+Useful+for+CI+pipelines%2C+agents%2C+and+automated+workflows.+Sync+the+latest+changes+or+keep+files+continuously+up+to+date.&amp;logoUrl=https%3A%2F%2Fpublish-01.obsidian.md%2Faccess%2Ff786db9fac45774fa4f0d8112e232d67%2Ffavicon-96x96.png&amp;siteName=Obsidian+Help' data-full='https://ogimage.obsidian.md/og-image.png?title=Headless+Sync&amp;description=Obsidian+Sync+offers+a+headless+client+to+sync+vaults+without+using+the+desktop+app.+Useful+for+CI+pipelines%2C+agents%2C+and+automated+workflows.+Sync+the+latest+changes+or+keep+files+continuously+up+to+date.&amp;logoUrl=https%3A%2F%2Fpublish-01.obsidian.md%2Faccess%2Ff786db9fac45774fa4f0d8112e232d67%2Ffavicon-96x96.png&amp;siteName=Obsidian+Help' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47336100' data-tags='Show HN,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47336100' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(158) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://channelsurfer.tv' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Channel Surfer – Watch YouTube like it’s cable TV</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: Channel Surfer — 像看有线电视一样观看 YouTube</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47336100' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 572</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47336100' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 167</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47336100' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Channel Surfer offers a TV-like, channel-based browsing UX for YouTube, making it feel like flipping through channels rather than scrolling feeds. It emphasizes curated discovery and continuous viewing without the usual YouTube UI clutter. The concept highlights how alternative interfaces can change how users consume long-form video on the web.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Channel Surfer 提供了类似有线电视的频道式浏览 UI，让 YouTube 的观看体验像在切换频道，而非刷订阅。通过更便捷的聚合与连续播放，它去除了常规界面的干扰，强调了对长视频的发现与连贯观看。这个想法展示了替代界面如何改变网络视频的消费方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://channelsurfer.tv/og-image.png' data-full='https://channelsurfer.tv/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47311815' data-tags='Design,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47311815' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(159) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://felixturner.github.io/hex-map-wfc/article/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Building a Procedural Hex Map with Wave Function Collapse</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用波函数坍缩生成六边形地图的程序化方法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47311815' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 567</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47311815' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 86</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47311815' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Using Wave Function Collapse to generate hex maps, this guide walks through tile constraints and edge matching for procedural world building. It also covers performance considerations and practical tips for integrating WFC into game tooling. The approach offers a reusable pattern for designers seeking deterministic, tweakable map generation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文展示如何将波函数坍缩算法应用于六边形网格的程序化地图生成，讲解瓷砖约束与边界匹配的要点。内容还讨论了性能考量及将 WFC 集成到游戏工具链的实用技巧。此方法为追求确定性、可调地图的设计者提供了可复用的模板。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://felixturner.github.io/hex-map-wfc/article/img/hero.jpg' data-full='https://felixturner.github.io/hex-map-wfc/article/img/hero.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47388676' data-tags='AI,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47388676' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(160) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sebastianraschka.com/llm-architecture-gallery/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LLM Architecture Gallery</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LLM 架构画廊</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47388676' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 566</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47388676' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47388676' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LLM Architecture Gallery presents a visual catalog of common and emerging LLM architectures, from vanilla transformer stacks to retrieval-augmented and modular designs. It helps engineers compare trade-offs in latency, memory, and accuracy. Takeaway: architecture choices shape capabilities and cost, and a visual guide helps map the design space.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LLM 架构画廊以可视化形式整理了常见与新兴的模型架构，从基础的 Transformer 堆栈到检索增强和模块化设计，帮助工程师对比在延迟、内存与精度等维度的权衡。要点：架构选择决定能力和成本，直观的对比有助于把握设计空间。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://sebastianraschka.com/llm-architecture-gallery/images/hero/architecture-gallery-hero.webp' data-full='https://sebastianraschka.com/llm-architecture-gallery/images/hero/architecture-gallery-hero.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47204571' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47204571' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(161) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://claude.com/import-memory' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Switch to Claude without starting over</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>无需重新开始即可切换到 Claude 的记忆导入</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47204571' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 563</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47204571' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 261</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47204571' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude now offers a memory import feature that lets you switch to Claude without losing prior context, easing migrations and preserving continuity. It reduces migration friction and supports ongoing workflows across models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude 新增记忆导入功能，切换至 Claude 时不丢失先前上下文，降低迁移摩擦并保持工作流连续性。为跨模型工作流提供了更顺畅的迁移体验。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47275035' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(162) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cjd98091g28o' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US economy unexpectedly sheds 92k jobs in February</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>二月美国经济意外减少9.2万个工作岗位</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47275035' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 562</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47275035' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 772</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47275035' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>February payroll data surprised to the downside, shedding about 92,000 jobs as hiring cooled across sectors. The softer labor market could influence Fed policy expectations and AI-driven productivity gains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>二月就业数据意外下降约9.2万个岗位，招聘在各领域放缓。这一趋势可能影响美联储的货币政策预期，并对 AI 驱动的生产力成长路径产生影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/6efa/live/41eb38e0-1962-11f1-a1cd-a71e69f1cf89.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/6efa/live/41eb38e0-1962-11f1-a1cd-a71e69f1cf89.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
