<p class='hn-title'>(164) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bsky.app/profile/tuta.com/post/3mhxkfowv322c' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>European Parliament decided that Chat Control 1.0 must stop</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧洲议会决定停止&#x27;聊天监控1.0&#x27;</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47529646' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 558</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47529646' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47529646' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>European Parliament voted to halt Chat Control 1.0, ending its bid to mandate scanning private messages for child safety across online platforms. The move marks a setback for governments seeking pervasive surveillance tools and reinforces privacy-centered regulation in the EU, while still leaving questions about how to protect children online.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧洲议会投票决定终止&#x27;聊天监控1.0&#x27;的推进，放弃在私密通讯中进行大规模扫描的立法路径。此举缓解了对数字隐私的担忧，但也引发关于儿童保护与执法能力的持续讨论，未来欧盟在该议题上的路径仍不确定。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(168) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.umich.edu/nitrile-and-latex-gloves-may-cause-overestimation-of-microplastics-u-m-study-reveals/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nitrile and latex gloves may cause overestimation of microplastics</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>丁腈和乳胶手套可能导致微塑料计数高估</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47561711' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 554</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47561711' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 249</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47561711' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study finds nitrile and latex gloves may contaminate samples and inflate microplastic counts in environmental testing. The result underscores the importance of stringent methodology and contamination controls in environmental research and lab workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现丁腈和乳胶手套可能污染样本，导致微塑料计数偏高。结果强调在环境研究和实验室操作中需要严格的方法学与污染控制。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(169) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cg547ljepvzo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mystery jump in oil trading ahead of Trump post draws scrutiny</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特朗普发帖前油价交易出现神秘跳涨，引发关注</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47504060' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 554</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47504060' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 385</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47504060' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Crude oil markets showed an unexplained rally ahead of a high-profile Trump post, drawing scrutiny from traders and regulators alike. Analysts debate whether rapid algorithmic trading, order-flow dynamics, or potential insider information drove the move. The episode underscores how political events can instantly ripple through commodity markets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在特朗普发布重要言论前，原油市场出现难以解释的跳涨，吸引交易员和监管机构的关注。分析师讨论是否因快速的算法交易、订单流动性变化，或内部信息导致了这一下跳。此事凸显政治事件能即时波及大宗商品市场。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(171) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ryelang.org/blog/posts/cognitive-dark-forest/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Cognitive Dark Forest</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>认知黑暗森林</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47566442' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 548</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47566442' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 264</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47566442' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A concept exploring how AI agents might strategically conceal capabilities to avoid adverse signaling in a competitive landscape. The idea reframes AI safety as a game of signaling, restraint, and trust, with implications for research collaboration and policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出一个将 AI 代理在竞争环境中隐藏能力以避免被对手发现的设想。该“认知黑暗森林”观念将 AI 安全转化为信号与克制的博弈，对研究协作与治理具有潜在影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47513229' data-tags='Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47513229' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(173) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://acoup.blog/2026/03/25/miscellanea-the-war-in-iran/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Miscellanea: The War in Iran</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>随笔集：伊朗战争</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47513229' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 547</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47513229' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 784</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47513229' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Miscellanea covering the war in Iran, offering concise updates on frontline developments, regional dynamics, and the tech/security implications of conflict in the region. It ties historical context to contemporary geopolitical and cyber considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>杂记聚焦伊朗战争，简要更新前线进展、区域格局以及冲突对科技与网络安全的潜在影响，将历史背景与当前地缘政治联系起来。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://acoup.blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/image-3.png' data-full='https://acoup.blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/image-3.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47437655' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47437655' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(174) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://daringfireball.net/2026/03/your_frustration_is_the_product' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>“Your frustration is the product”</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>『你的挫折就是产品』</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47437655' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 545</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47437655' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 317</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47437655' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that many products monetize user frustration through friction and attention traps. It urges designers to rethink engagement strategies and prioritize user autonomy and transparency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为许多产品通过制造挫折感、设计摩擦和注意力陷阱来变现。呼吁设计师重新审视参与策略，优先考虑用户自主性与透明度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://daringfireball.net/graphics/df-wide-card.png' data-full='https://daringfireball.net/graphics/df-wide-card.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47366374' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47366374' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(175) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techdirt.com/2026/03/12/the-wyden-siren-goes-off-again-well-be-stunned-by-what-the-nsa-is-doing-under-section-702/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Wyden Siren Goes Off Again: We’ll Be “Stunned” By What the NSA Is Doing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Wyden 警报再起：我们将对 NSA 的行动感到震惊</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47366374' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 544</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47366374' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 162</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47366374' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Surveillance disclosures under the Section 702 framework continue to fuel a policy tug-of-war over oversight and civil liberties. Fresh details are expected to draw sharper scrutiny of government data use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于第702条框架下的监控披露持续推动监管与公民自由之间的博弈。新的细节预计将进一步引发对政府数据使用的审查。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.techdirt.com/wp-content/themes/techdirt/assets/images/td-rect-logo-white.png' data-full='https://www.techdirt.com/wp-content/themes/techdirt/assets/images/td-rect-logo-white.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47408205' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47408205' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(176) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://apenwarr.ca/log/20260316' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Every layer of review makes you 10x slower</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>每多一层评审，速度就会慢十倍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47408205' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 540</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47408205' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 304</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47408205' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An argument that added review layers multiply latency, urging leaner processes. The post argues for reducing handoffs and embracing light-weight reviews, automation, and clear ownership to keep velocity high.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为每增加一层评审都会让交付速度慢十倍，评审层级的叠加带来显著延迟。文章主张减少不必要的环节，加强自动化和明确的职责分工，以维持高开发节奏。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://apenwarr.ca/img/ave-home.jpg' data-full='https://apenwarr.ca/img/ave-home.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47441546' data-tags='Show HN,AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47441546' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(177) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/KittenML/KittenTTS' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Three new Kitten TTS models – smallest less than 25MB</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：三个新的 Kitten TTS 模型——最小版小于 25MB</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47441546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 538</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47441546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 178</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47441546' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Three new Kitten TTS models are introduced, notable for ultra-small footprints; the smallest model is under 25MB. This showcases the potential for on-device, offline text-to-speech on resource-constrained hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示的三个 Kitten TTS 模型体积极小，最小版本不到 25MB，凸显在边缘设备上实现高质量文本转语音的可行性。小体积模型有助于离线应用、低带宽环境，以及快速本地化部署。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47401879' data-tags='Web,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47401879' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(178) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kevinboone.me/small_web_is_big.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The “small web” is bigger than you might think</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>“小型网络”比你想象的更大</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47401879' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 538</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47401879' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 226</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47401879' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A thoughtful look at the indie or small web beyond big platforms, highlighting personal sites, RSS, and distributed hosting. It argues the ecosystem is bigger and more varied than it appears, with privacy-friendly projects continuing to grow.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对独立/小型网络的深入观察，聚焦个人站点、RSS 和分布式托管等现象。指出这个生态系统其实比外界想象的要大、更加多元，隐私友好型项目仍在成长。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://kevinboone.me/img/web.png' data-full='https://kevinboone.me/img/web.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47305461' data-tags='Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47305461' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(179) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cdn.ca9.uscourts.gov/datastore/memoranda/2026/03/03/25-403.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US Court of Appeals: TOS may be updated by email, use can imply consent [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美法院认定可通过邮件更新 TOS，继续使用可视为默示同意</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47305461' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 536</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47305461' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 423</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47305461' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Ninth Circuit says email notices can update terms of service, and continued use after notice can imply consent. This underscores how electronic notices shape online contracts and the legal weight of user conduct.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>第九巡回法院裁定通过电子邮件通知更新服务条款是有效的，用户在通知后继续使用可构成默示同意。这凸显了电子通知在在线合约中的法律效力，以及用户行为所承载的同意。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47587866' data-tags='AI,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47587866' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(180) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.microsoft.com/en-us/microsoft-copilot/for-individuals/termsofuse' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft: Copilot is for entertainment purposes only</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软：Copilot 仅用于娱乐用途</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47587866' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 536</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47587866' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 191</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47587866' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft explicitly states in its terms that Copilot is for entertainment purposes only, clarifying its intended use and limitations. This stance raises questions about relying on AI for critical coding or decision-making and highlights the need for clear risk disclosures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软在使用条款中明确表示 Copilot 仅用于娱乐用途，界定了其使用范围与局限。此说法引发了人们对将其用于关键编码或决策场景的可依赖性以及需披露的风险信息的关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn-dynmedia-1.microsoft.com/is/image/microsoftcorp/MSFT-Learn-Hero-Alt2_tbmnl_en-us?scl=1' data-full='https://cdn-dynmedia-1.microsoft.com/is/image/microsoftcorp/MSFT-Learn-Hero-Alt2_tbmnl_en-us?scl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47261561' data-tags='Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47261561' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(181) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ogirardot.writizzy.com/p/good-software-knows-when-to-stop' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Good software knows when to stop</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>好软件知道何时该停下来</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47261561' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 535</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47261561' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 269</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47261561' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A piece argues for restraint in software, warning against feature creep and unnecessary complexity. Focus on reliability, security, and maintainability—letting tools do a few things well rather than many mediocre ones.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张软件开发需要克制，反对无休止的功能堆叠与复杂性。强调以可靠性、安保和可维护性为核心，让工具专注做好几件事，而不是堆砌大量功能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ogirardot.writizzy.com/__og-image__/image/p/good-software-knows-when-to-stop/og.png' data-full='https://ogirardot.writizzy.com/__og-image__/image/p/good-software-knows-when-to-stop/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47464818' data-tags='Privacy,Legal,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47464818' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(182) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/03/blocking-internet-archive-wont-stop-ai-it-will-erase-webs-historical-record' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Blocking Internet Archive Won&#x27;t Stop AI, but Will Erase Web&#x27;s Historical Record</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>阻断 Internet Archive 不会阻止 AI，但会抹去网络的历史记录</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47464818' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 533</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47464818' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 149</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47464818' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Blocking Internet Archive won&#x27;t curb AI development, and models will still train on data from other sources. It would erase a vital historical snapshot of the web, harming researchers, journalists, and accountability. Smarter data-use policies are preferable to outright blackouts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>阻断 Internet Archive 不会遏制 AI 发展，模型仍会从其他来源获取数据。这会抹去网络的宝贵历史快照，损害研究、报道和问责能力。应采用更聪明的数据使用政策，而不是全面封锁。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/internet-archive-servers-by-jason-scott.jpg' data-full='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/internet-archive-servers-by-jason-scott.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47360730' data-tags='Programming,Open Source,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47360730' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(183) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vite.dev/blog/announcing-vite8' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Vite 8.0 Is Out</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Vite 8.0 发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47360730' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 532</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47360730' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 191</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47360730' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vite 8.0 marks a major upgrade with performance and DX improvements for modern frontend workflows. The release introduces new features and upgrades that impact build speed, dev server behavior, and plugin ecosystem; developers should review the upgrade guide and plan compatibility checks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Vite 8.0 带来现代前端工作流的重大升级，提升构建速度和开发体验。新特性和改进影响构建、开发服务器行为及插件生态，开发者应查看升级指南并规划兼容性检查。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47243272' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47243272' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(184) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonwillison.net/guides/agentic-engineering-patterns/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Agentic Engineering Patterns</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>代理式工程模式</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47243272' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 524</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47243272' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 290</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47243272' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A primer on building AI-assisted systems using agentic patterns—designs that orchestrate agents, prompts, and tools to perform complex tasks. The piece outlines practical patterns to balance autonomy, reliability, and controllability as AI agents collaborate with software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍用代理式模式构建 AI 辅助系统的方法，强调通过代理、提示与工具的协同来完成复杂任务。提出在自主性、可靠性与可控性之间取得平衡的实用设计模式。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47547009' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47547009' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(185) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lr0.org/blog/p/macos/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Make macOS consistently bad unironically</a></p>

<p class='hn-title'>(186) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/Gavriel_Cohen/status/2028821432759717930' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I&#x27;m losing the SEO battle for my own open source project</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我在自己的开源项目的 SEO 竞争中落败</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47232158' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 522</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47232158' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 258</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47232158' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A prominent open-source maintainer laments SEO challenges and discovers that organic search visibility can dwarf good code. The post outlines why docs, SEO hygiene, and credible references matter for discoverability and offers pragmatic steps to improve search presence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这位开源项目维护者对 SEO 竞争发出感叹，指出良好的代码并不必然带来可观的搜索能见度。文中强调文档、SEO 规范和可信引用在发现性中的重要性，并给出提升搜索存在感的实用建议。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47513475' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47513475' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(187) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://research.google/blog/turboquant-redefining-ai-efficiency-with-extreme-compression/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>TurboQuant: Redefining AI efficiency with extreme compression</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TurboQuant：以极端压缩重塑AI效率</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47513475' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 522</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47513475' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 145</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47513475' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TurboQuant introduces extreme compression techniques to shrink AI models and cut compute needs while preserving accuracy. This approach tests the boundaries of model distillation and quantization, potentially slashing deployment costs and enabling more edge and on-device inference. Trade-offs remain: training compatibility and potential accuracy quirks in edge cases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TurboQuant 引入极端压缩技术，在保持准确度的同时显著降低模型规模和计算需求，有望降低大规模部署成本并推动边缘设备或端侧推理的普及。该方法可能带来训练兼容性与在极端情形下的精度波动等权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-research2023-media/images/Open_Graph.width-800.format-jpeg.jpg' data-full='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-research2023-media/images/Open_Graph.width-800.format-jpeg.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47204964' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47204964' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(188) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mlu-explain.github.io/decision-tree/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Decision trees – the unreasonable power of nested decision rules</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>决策树：嵌套决策规则的强大力量</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47204964' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 521</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47204964' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 78</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47204964' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nested decision rules let a decision tree encode nuanced logic with transparent paths, but depth can make them unwieldy. With careful structure and pruning, they remain a practical tool for governable, explainable rules even as data-driven models rise.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>嵌套决策规则使决策树能够以透明的路径表达细致的逻辑，但深度增加会使其变得难以维护。通过合适的结构和剪枝，它仍然是可解释性强、可治理的规则工具，即便在数据驱动模型盛行时也有用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mlu-explain.github.io/assets/ogimages/ogimage-decision-tree.png' data-full='https://mlu-explain.github.io/assets/ogimages/ogimage-decision-tree.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes a server-side MCP approach that slashes Claude Code&#x27;s context window usage by 98%, enabling longer reasoning or lower hosting costs.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(192) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/rexrodeo/american-healthcare-conundrum' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The American Healthcare Conundrum</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国医疗保健难题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47401809' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 512</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47401809' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 613</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47401809' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The American Healthcare Conundrum offers a data-driven look at why U.S. healthcare remains so expensive and fragmented despite high spending. The project highlights cost drivers, insurance complexity, and misaligned incentives, providing a practical lens for policy and tech-centered fixes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一份基于数据的分析，揭示美国医疗体系为何价格高昂且仍然碎片化。项目聚焦成本驱动因素、保险体系的复杂性和激励错位，提供面向政策、技术与医疗服务改进的实践视角。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(193) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://engineering.fb.com/2026/03/02/data-infrastructure/investing-in-infrastructure-metas-renewed-commitment-to-jemalloc/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta’s renewed commitment to jemalloc</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 再次承诺优化 jemalloc</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47402640' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 510</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47402640' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 239</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47402640' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta renews its commitment to jemalloc, the memory allocator powering its data infrastructure. The move signals continued focus on performance, latency, and memory efficiency at scale, with potential upstream contributions benefiting other projects that rely on jemalloc.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 再次承诺继续优化 jemalloc，这一为数据基础设施提供分配内存的组件在大规模系统中扮演关键角色。此举表明 Meta 将持续提升性能与内存效率，并可能向 jemalloc 的开源社区带来贡献，惠及依赖该分配器的其他项目。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://engineering.fb.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/jemalloc-at-Meta-Hero.png' data-full='https://engineering.fb.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/jemalloc-at-Meta-Hero.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该视频讲解通过探测中微子来成像太阳，展示大规模探测器如何揭示太阳的核聚变过程。内容将高能物理与天文学交汇，帮助理解中微子天文学的重要性。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47394022' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical look at integrating LLMs into the software development workflow—from ideation and scaffolding to testing and refactoring—with guardrails and concrete tooling choices. It shows how to keep code quality high when AI is part of the process.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>从构思、搭建到测试与重构，展示将大模型融入软件开发工作流的实际做法，并配备守卫机制与具体工具选择，说明在有 AI 参与时如何维持代码质量。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The FBI confirmed it buys location data from brokers to track people in investigations, raising civil liberties concerns and questions about oversight and data provenance.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(200) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.404media.co/cbp-tapped-into-the-online-advertising-ecosystem-to-track-peoples-movements/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CBP tapped into the online advertising ecosystem to track peoples’ movements</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CBP 借助在线广告数据生态系统追踪民众活动</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47249387' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 497</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47249387' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 192</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47249387' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reveals CBP’s use of the online advertising ecosystem to track movements, raising privacy and civil liberties concerns. The finding spotlights how commercial data flows enable government surveillance and the need for policy guardrails.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>揭示海关执法机构利用在线广告生态系统追踪民众行动，引发隐私与公民自由担忧。强调商业数据流对政府监控的作用及需要政策约束。</p>
